High-quality TPU kernel optimization remains a challenging task, and we release the JAXBench benchmark, evaluation harness, and baseline results to support open source contributions.

</details>


### [82] [DC-Leap: Training-Free Acceleration of dLLMs via Draft-Guided Contiguous Leaping Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20467)
*Yanhua Jiao,Tianyi Wu,Xiaoxi Sun,Yulin Li,HuiLing Zhen,Libo Qin,Baotian Hu,Zhuotao Tian,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DC-Leap，一种无需训练的加速扩散大语言模型 (dLLM) 推理的框架，通过动态连续验证策略消除联合概率依赖误差 (JPDE)，并结合草稿引导解码实现长序列生成高达 53.19x（结合 KV-Cache 可达 105.02x）的加速，同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 dLLM 的并行解码受制于过度保守的置信度阈值，这源于联合概率依赖误差 (JPDE)，导致冗余去噪迭代和次优推理速度。

Method: DC-Leap 引入动态连续验证策略，将严格有序的因果约束融入并行解码过程，通过逐步验证 token 依赖有效中和 JPDE；同时采用草稿引导解码机制，利用草稿跨多 token 前向跳跃扩展上下文，提供前瞻上下文并保留双向注意力的结构优势。

Result: 在标准基准测试中，DC-Leap 实现显著加速：MBPP 长序列生成最高 53.19x，结合 KV-Cache 最高 105.02x，生成质量相当。

Conclusion: DC-Leap 通过无训练的动态验证与草稿引导机制，成功在中等置信度区间可靠加速 dLLM 推理，大幅提升长序列生成效率且不牺牲质量，为扩散语言模型实际部署提供实用方案。

Abstract: While parallel decoding is central to the efficiency of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), current strategies are often hindered by overly conservative confidence thresholds. These thresholds, necessitated by the Joint Probability Dependence Error (JPDE), result in redundant denoising iterations and suboptimal inference speeds. To overcome this, we propose DC-Leap, a training-free framework that enables reliable acceleration of dLLMs in the moderate-confidence regime. DC-Leap introduces a Dynamic Contiguous Verification strategy that integrates strictly-ordered causal constraints into the parallel decoding process. By progressively validating token dependencies, this mechanism effectively neutralizes the JPDE, enabling reliable acceleration with comparable performance. Furthermore, DC-Leap incorporates the draft-guided decoding mechanism, where the draft helps extend the context by leaping forward across multiple tokens, providing look-ahead context and retaining the structural benefits of bidirectional attention during inference. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DC-Leap achieves substantial speedups, up to 53.19x on MBPP for long-sequence generation, and up to 105.02x when combined with KV-Cache with comparable generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/ffh-wyls/DC-Leap .

</details>


### [83] [Incomplete Prompt Jailbreaks in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20473)
*Yeonjea Kim,Bumjin Park,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出不完整提示越狱（IPJ）概念，系统实证分析不完整有害提示如何诱发有害续写，发现LLM会系统性延迟拒绝直到句子结束，参数微调无法跨域泛化，进而识别出终止神经元和继续神经元，指出神经元级干预可实现更精确鲁棒的IPJ防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重大语言模型虽部署了安全防护，但句子补全任务仍易受不完整有害提示攻击，缺乏系统性表征及有效泛化防御手段，需深入研究攻击机理并探索更细粒度的防御策略。

Method: 将现象形式化为不完整提示越狱（IPJ），通过实证研究表征不完整提示何时及如何引发有害续写，分析多样吸引子类型，揭示LLM延迟拒绝至句子终止的行为模式；实验验证参数微调在跨内容域和吸引子类型上的泛化失效；进而识别终止神经元与继续神经元，阐明其在句子补全中的功能角色。

Result: LLM系统性地将拒绝推迟到句子终止；针对不完整有害提示的参数微调无法跨内容域和吸引子类型泛化；识别出的终止神经元和继续神经元在句子补全中扮演关键角色，神经元级干预展现出更精确、鲁棒的IPJ防御潜力。

Conclusion: 不完整提示越狱揭示了开放权重LLM的系统性安全缺口，传统参数微调防御不足，神经元级功能定位与干预为构建细粒度、泛化性强的安全防护提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly released as open-weight models with safeguards against harmful requests. Nevertheless, sentence completion remains vulnerable to incomplete harmful prompts. In this work, we formalize this phenomenon as incomplete prompt jailbreaks (IPJ) and provide a systematic empirical characterization of when and how incomplete prompts elicit harmful continuations. We analyze diverse attractor types associated with incomplete sentence continuation and show that LLMs systematically delay refusal until sentence termination. We further demonstrate that training models to refuse incomplete harmful prompts via parameter tuning is insufficient, failing to generalize across both content domains and attractor types. To enable fine-grained control, we identify two functional neurons: termination and continuation neurons. By clarifying their roles in sentence completion, we highlight the potential of neuron-level interventions for more precise and robust IPJ defenses.

</details>


### [84] [VeriSimpl: Robust Optimization Modeling from Natural Language using Simplification-based Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20474)
*Sumaya Abdul Rahman,Seckhen Ariel Andrade Cuellar,Ghani Raissov,Mohammad Raza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一种基于简化验证的求解器LLM框架VeriSimpl，用于自然语言到优化建模的鲁棒形式化，通过求解器生成简化诊断查询让LLM推理正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言到优化建模的方法难以保证推导出的形式化正确实现预期任务，即使无错误执行也可能存在语义偏差。

Method: 提出简化验证策略，利用优化求解器生成关于候选形式化的简化诊断查询，沿约束和决策变量维度简化，使LLM能在固定全局上下文中局部推理正确性。

Result: 在多个优化基准上评估，显示相较现有方法准确率持续提升，并提供新颖的高精度自我验证信号。

Conclusion: VeriSimpl框架通过简化验证有效提升了自然语言到优化建模的鲁棒性和准确性，为LLM辅助优化建模提供了可靠的自我验证机制。

Abstract: Natural language interfaces can greatly benefit the accessibility and usability of optimization modeling, and recent advances in large language models (LLMs) show promise in automatically translating textual problem descriptions into executable solver formulations. However, a key challenge for existing approaches is to ensure that the inferred formulation correctly implements the intended task, even if it may execute without errors. We introduce VeriSimpl, a solver LLM framework for robust natural-language-to-optimization formalization. Our approach is based on the idea of simplification-based verification, where the optimization solver is leveraged to generate simplified diagnostic queries about a candidate formulation to allow the LLM to tractably reason about the correctness of the formulation with respect to the task description. We present such simplification strategies along different dimensions with respect to problem constraints and decision variables, which allow the LLM to reason locally under fixed global contexts. Evaluations on a range of optimization benchmarks show how our approach provides consistent improvements in accuracy over existing methods, while also providing a novel high-precision self-verification signal.

</details>


### [85] [Agentic Context Management: Solving Agent Memory and Cost by Treating Them as Lifecycle and Architecture Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21503)
*Gaurav Dadhich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了 Agentic Context Management (ACM) 这一新学科，旨在解决生产级 AI Agent 因上下文管理不善（如对话历史膨胀、Token 成本二次方增长、跨会话遗忘）而导致的推理失效问题。ACM 将上下文管理视为完整生命周期，拆解为五大原语：架构设计、摄入、作用域划分、预判、压缩与巩固。作者给出了经济性论证：朴素累积导致二次方 Token 成本，粗略摘要虽线性但准确率断崖，唯有验证式压缩可在保真度下实现线性成本。参考实现 Maximem Synap 在 LongMemEval (92%) 和 LoCoMo (93.2%) 上达标，并指出现有基准尚未覆盖的延迟、Token 效率、上下文腐烂抵抗力以及决策级/组织级上下文等前沿维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的 AI Agent 失效主要源于无法有效管理推理上下文（对话历史、大型提示词、工具定义、工具输出膨胀），导致 Token 成本随轮次二次方增长、跨会话遗漏关键信息。现有主流方案将其视为单纯的存储与检索问题，视野过窄。

Method: 提出 Agentic Context Management (ACM) 学科，将上下文管理建模为生命周期，分解为五大原语：1) 架构设计：决定记住什么、如何结构化；2) 摄入：抽取并结构化信息；3) 作用域划分：按数据类型选择存储，跨组织层级管理；4) 预判：判断当前相关性并预测未来需求；5) 压缩与巩固：在预算内压缩上下文且保留关键信息及溯源。并给出经济性模型对比三种策略的成本-准确率权衡。

Result: 参考实现 Maximem Synap 作为多租户服务落地五大原语，在 LongMemEval 达到 92%，在 LoCoMo 达到 93.2%（详见第 6 节配置）。验证了验证式压缩可在保持高保真的前提下实现线性 Token 成本。

Conclusion: ACM 重新定义了生产级 Agent 的上下文管理，从存储检索升级为全生命周期工程。现有基准尚未覆盖延迟、Token 效率、上下文腐烂抵抗力及决策级/组织级上下文等关键维度，未来研究应向这些前沿推进。

Abstract: Production AI agents' failures are less often due to an inability to reason well and more often because they cannot manage what is in their reasoning context: conversation histories, large prompts, large tool definitions, and ballooning tool outputs. Agents drown in their own accumulating history while paying a token cost that grows every turn, producing missing recalls within and across conversations. The incumbent response treats this as a storage-and-retrieval problem. We argue that framing is too narrow. Actively managing what an agent holds in mind is a lifecycle, not merely a store: it spans deciding what to remember, extracting and structuring it, choosing the right store per data type, consolidating and forgetting while preserving provenance, deciding what is relevant now, anticipating what is needed next, and compacting context to a budget without losing what matters. In serious production this operates not over a single user but across an organizational scope hierarchy. We name this discipline Agentic Context Management (ACM) and decompose it into five primitives: architecting, ingesting, scoping, anticipating, and compacting & consolidation. We then make the economic case: naive context accumulation grows token cost quadratically in conversation length, crude summarization buys linear cost at the price of an accuracy cliff, and only validated compaction achieves linear cost with preserved fidelity. We describe a reference implementation, Maximem Synap, that realizes the five primitives as a multi-tenant service and reports 92% on LongMemEval and 93.2% on LoCoMo under the configuration detailed in Section 6. We close with dimensions existing benchmarks do not yet capture, latency, token efficiency, and context-rot resistance, and the frontier of decision-level and organization-level context the category points toward.

</details>


### [86] [SonicSampler: Unified Tile-Aware Kernels for LLM Sampling and Speculative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20475)
*Pragaash Ponnusamy,Shivam Sahni,Jue Wang,Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SonicSampler 是一个统一的采样内核套件，垂直融合了完整的采样流水线（logit 处理、token 选择、推测验证），支持动态逐请求采样行为（语法约束、惩罚、logit bias、温度缩放、top-k/top-p/min-p 过滤等），并完全兼容 CUDA Graph。核心创新是分层两阶段 top-k 算法，利用 LLM 输出的低熵结构实现大词表高效选择，在竞争基线上达到 10 倍加速，在异构推测解码工作负载上达到 16 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推理采样实现存在三大局限：仅加速流水线子集、依赖多个内核启动、假设批次内采样行为同质。这些限制阻碍了对动态服务工作负载的支持，并阻止了高效的 CUDA Graph 执行。

Method: 提出 SonicSampler，基于 tile-aware Triton 内核，将完整采样流水线垂直融合为固定的、工作负载感知的执行模型。支持语法约束解码、重复/频率/存在惩罚、logit bias、温度缩放、top-k/top-p/min-p 过滤及推测验证等动态逐请求行为，均在单一批次内核中完成。核心是新颖的分层两阶段 top-k 算法，利用 LLM 输出的低熵特性，在大词表上实现高效选择。

Result: 分层两阶段 top-k 算法在竞争基线上实现高达 10 倍加速。在异构推测解码工作负载上，SonicSampler 整体实现高达 16 倍加速，同时保持灵活的批次执行能力。

Conclusion: SonicSampler 通过统一融合内核和分层 top-k 算法，解决了现有采样实现的碎片化和性能瓶颈，为动态 LLM 服务提供了高效、CUDA Graph 兼容的采样方案，显著提升了推测解码等异构工作负载的吞吐率。

Abstract: Sampling in LLM inference comprises a combinatorial set of logit processing, token selection, and verification operations for speculative decoding. However, existing implementations either accelerate only subsets of this pipeline, rely on multiple kernel launches, or assume homogeneous sampling behavior across a batch, limiting support for dynamic serving workloads and preventing efficient CUDA Graph execution. We present $\textbf{SonicSampler}$, a unified suite of tile-aware Triton kernels that vertically fuses the complete sampling pipeline into a fixed, workload-aware execution model. Our kernels support dynamic per-request sampling behaviors, including grammar-constrained decoding, repetition, frequency and presence penalties, logit bias, temperature scaling, top-$k$ / top-$p$ / min-$p$ filtering, and speculative verification - within a single batched kernel while remaining fully CUDA Graph-compatible. Central to our approach is a novel hierarchical two-stage top-$k$ algorithm that achieves up to $\textbf{10x speedup}$ over competitive baselines and exploits the low-entropy structure of LLM outputs to enable efficient selection over large vocabularies. Across heterogeneous speculative decoding workloads, SonicSampler achieves up to $\textbf{16x speedup}$ over state-of-the-art baselines while preserving flexible batched execution.

</details>


### [87] [Benchmarking Large Language Models on Multi-Sensor Physical Hazard Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20476)
*Faizan Iqbal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对五大主流大语言模型（ChatGPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Flash、DeepSeek、Kimi、Llama 3.1 8B）进行实证基准测试，评估其对多传感器物理危害数据的判断能力。结果显示：所有模型在多传感器联合评估场景（Category A）上得分极低（近零），但在单传感器阈值违规场景（Category B）上表现近乎完美。结构化表格格式无稳定优势，ChatGPT-4o在纯文本提示下表现显著更好。这对在物理安全监控系统中部署LLM有直接启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在工业安全、环境监测等物理系统中的应用增加，迫切需要了解LLM能否正确处理多传感器同时升高但未超单一阈值的复杂危害模式，即评估其在“多传感器联合评估”、“响应比例性”和“模式消歧”三类场景下的可靠性，为实际部署提供实证依据。

Method: 构建包含60个场景的实证基准，涵盖三大类别：多传感器联合评估、响应比例性、模式消歧。对五个模型进行1800次API调用（temperature=0.0），对比结构化表格格式与纯文本提示两种输入方式，量化模型在各类场景下的准确率得分。

Result: 所有五个模型在Category A多传感器场景上得分极低（Q2: 0.000-0.208；Q3: 0.000-0.592），但在Category B单传感器场景上达到近完美准确率（Q1: 0.975-1.000）。表格格式无一致优势；ChatGPT-4o在纯文本提示下表现显著优于表格格式（p=0.001）。模型普遍无法在多传感器同时升高但未超个别安全限时发出预防性预警。

Conclusion: 当前主流LLM在物理安全监控的核心任务——多传感器联合异常检测上存在系统性缺陷，无法识别“单一传感器未超限但多传感器协同升高”这一典型前兆模式。直接将这类模型部署于安全关键系统存在重大风险，需开发专门的多模态融合架构或引入显式规则引擎作为补充。

Abstract: We present an empirical benchmark evaluating how five large language models assess multisensor physical hazard data. Testing 60 scenarios across three categories - multi-sensor joint assessment, response proportionality, and pattern disambiguation - with 1,800 API calls at temperature 0.0, we find that all tested models consistently produced no precautionary warning signal across the tested scenarios where multiple sensors are simultaneously elevated below their individual safety limits, while achieving near-perfect accuracy on single-sensor threshold violations. All five models (ChatGPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, DeepSeek, Kimi, Llama 3.1 8B) score near zero on Category A multi-sensor scenarios (Q2: 0.000-0.208; Q3: 0.000-0.592) compared to strong performance on single-sensor scenarios (Category B Q1: 0.975-1.000). Structured tabular formatting shows no consistent advantage over plain prose; ChatGPT-4o performs significantly better under prose (p = 0.001). These findings have direct implications for practitioners deploying the tested models in physical safety monitoring systems.

</details>


### [88] [Semi-Supervised Text-Attributed Graph Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20477)
*Yurui Lai,Samir Moustafa,Renchi Yang,Tsz Nam Chan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为WSD的统一半监督框架，用于文本属性图(TAG)的表示学习，通过图文协同编码、Wasserstein距离引导的图草图算法和LLM文本合成模块，解决了现有方法在可扩展性、模态融合、标签稀缺和人类可读文本生成方面的不足，在基准数据集上实现了最先进的性能-压缩权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TAG表示学习方法在与大语言模型(LLM)结合时面临严重的可扩展性瓶颈；数据蒸馏虽是有前景的以数据为中心的解决方案，但现有方法无法捕捉图与文本模态间的复杂交互、难以应对半监督设置中的标签稀缺问题，且缺乏为下游LLM任务生成人类可读文本属性的能力。

Method: 提出WSD框架，包含三大核心模块：1) 图文协同编码模块，采用双路径编码器(图感知和图自由)在协同自训练方案中获取可靠伪标签并融合互补图文特征；2) 基于Wasserstein距离的理论奠基图草图算法；3) 成本效益高的LLM文本合成模块，利用基于聚类的关键词提取为浓缩节点生成连贯、人类可读的摘要。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，WSD在GNN和LLM基础的下游任务上均实现了最先进的性能-压缩权衡，验证了其在有效且高效的TAG学习或分析方面的优越性。

Conclusion: WSD框架通过统一的半监督学习范式，结合图文协同编码、Wasserstein距离引导的图压缩和LLM驱动的文本合成，成功解决了TAG学习中的可扩展性、模态融合、标签稀缺和可读性生成等关键挑战，为大规模文本属性图的高效表示学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: {\em Text-Attributed Graphs} (TAGs) have emerged as an expressive data model for integrating graph topology with rich textual semantics. Existing representation learning methods over TAGs suffer from severe scalability bottlenecks, particularly together with {\em Large Language Models} (LLMs). While data distillation offers a promising data-centric solution, existing methods fail to capture the complex interplay between graph and text modalities, struggle with the label scarcity inherent in semi-supervised settings, and lack the ability to produce the human-readable textual attributes required for downstream LLM-based tasks.
  To address these challenges, we propose \algo{}, a unified semi-supervised framework guided by the {\em Wasserstein Distance} (WSD). Grounded in our empirical findings on real TAGs, \algo{} introduces a graph-text collaborative encoding module that utilizes dual-pathway encoders (graph-aware and -free) within a collaborative self-training scheme to harvest reliable pseudo-labels and fuse complementary graph-text features. Furthermore, we develop a theoretically grounded WSD-based graph sketching algorithm and a cost-effective LLM text synthesis module, which leverages cluster-based keyword extraction to generate coherent, human-readable summaries for condensed nodes. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that \algo{} achieves a state-of-the-art performance-compression trade-off in terms of both GNN- and LLM-based downstream tasks, enabling effective and efficient TAG learning or analytics.

</details>


### [89] [Beyond Liars' Bench: The Impact of Lie Typology, Depth, and Sparsity on Deception Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20479)
*Amr Moustafa,Max Feser,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 系统研究了影响大语言模型欺骗检测探针性能的多种因素，发现最优表示深度高度依赖数据集，更有表达力的探针仅带来选择性增益，稀疏自编码器特征与密集隐藏状态效果相当，而训练数据的谎言类型学对可检测性影响最大，揭示欺骗检测是高度依赖表示的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练探针检测大语言模型欺骗输出的方法在out-of-domain场景下失效，即在一种谎言类型上训练的探针难以迁移到其他类型的欺骗场景，因此需要系统研究各种因素对检测性能的影响。

Method: 构建包含制造、遗漏、夸大等多种欺骗类型的补充数据集增强标准基准，系统分析表示深度、探针表达能力、稀疏特征表示、训练数据谎言类型学四个因素，在七种探针类型上进行实验评估。

Result: 最优表示深度高度依赖数据集；更有表达力的探针相对于线性基线仅提供选择性增益；稀疏自编码器特征性能与密集隐藏状态相似；训练数据和谎言类型的选择显著改变欺骗的可检测性。

Conclusion: 欺骗检测是一个高度依赖表示的问题，训练数据的谎言类型学是决定检测性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Training probes to detect deceptive outputs from large language models is still an open problem. Recent work has demonstrated that detection probes fail especially in out-of-domain scenarios -- training on one type of lie does not transfer well to deception scenarios involving other types of lies. In this work, we conduct a systematic study on how various factors impact detection performance: representation depth, probe expressivity, sparse feature representations, and the lie typology of the training data. To this end, we augment standard benchmark training data with a supplementary dataset containing diverse types of deception, including fabrication, omission, and exaggeration examples. Analyzing these factors across seven probe types, our experimental results show that the optimal representation depth is highly dataset-dependent, more expressive probes provide only selective gains over linear baselines, and sparse autoencoder features perform similarly to dense hidden states. Ultimately, we demonstrate that the choice of training data and lie typology substantially changes detectability, highlighting that deception detection is a highly representation-dependent problem.

</details>


### [90] [Enabling Scalable Topology Inference in Distribution Systems via Constrained Multi-Source Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20480)
*Haoran Li,Lihao Mai,Muhao Guo,Jiaqi Wu,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于约束推理的配电网拓扑识别框架，利用异构证据修正基础拓扑，结合空间可行性和物理运行约束，通过局部重连和迭代求解实现可扩展的高精度拓扑重构，并引入伪证驱动的可靠性指标评估连接置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑识别方法主要依赖电气相似性或空间记录，在密集馈线和不一致元数据条件下不可靠，难以维护准确的配电网拓扑记录。

Method: 将拓扑识别建模为约束推理问题：利用公用事业提供的基础拓扑，结合异构证据（电气测量、空间数据、运行约束）检测不一致分配，在约束邻域内执行局部重连以保证可扩展性，迭代强制物理可行性生成运行一致的拓扑估计；引入伪证驱动的可靠性指标评估每个推断连接相对于替代可行分配的支持强度。

Result: 在三个包含8000多个AMI电表的馈线运行数据上验证，拓扑重构准确率超过95%，计算量显著低于全局推理方法；表明单纯相关性方法在密集城市馈线中产生模糊分配，而结合电气测量与空间运行约束可实现稳健可扩展的拓扑恢复。

Conclusion: 该框架通过约束推理和局部优化有效解决了配电网拓扑识别中的可扩展性和鲁棒性问题，为公用事业提供了可操作的拓扑验证优先级工具，在真实部署条件下实现了高精度、低计算成本的拓扑重构。

Abstract: Accurate distribution system topology is essential for outage localization, voltage analytics, and operation of distribution grids, yet maintaining reliable connectivity records remains challenging in practice due to heterogeneous and imperfect utility data. Existing topology identification methods often rely primarily on electrical similarity or spatial records alone, which become unreliable in dense feeders and under inconsistent metadata conditions. This paper formulates distribution topology identification as a constrained inference problem that refines a utility-provided base topology using heterogeneous evidence while enforcing spatial feasibility and physical operational constraints. Instead of reconstructing connectivity from scratch, the proposed framework detects inconsistent assignments, performs localized reconnection within constrained neighborhoods to ensure scalability, and iteratively enforces physical feasibility to produce operationally consistent topology estimates. In addition, a falsification-driven reliability metric evaluates how strongly each inferred connection is supported relative to alternative feasible assignments, enabling utilities to prioritize verification efforts while preserving system-wide observability. The framework is validated using operational data from three feeders comprising more than $8{,}000$ AMI meters in collaboration with a large U.S. utility. Results demonstrate over $95\%$ topology reconstruction accuracy while significantly reducing computational effort compared with global inference approaches. The study further shows that correlation-based methods alone produce ambiguous assignments in dense urban feeders, whereas combining electrical measurements with spatial and operational constraints enables robust and scalable topology recovery under realistic deployment conditions.

</details>


### [91] [Routing Without Training: Controllable-Ratio LLM Offloading via Reliability Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20481)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Kevin S Chan,Su Wang,Christopher Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARGO 是一种无需训练的本地-云协作路由框架，利用本地模型自身推理时的响应一致性作为路由信号，通过提示变采样、贝叶斯早停和部署时校准，在多种推理和问答任务上超越无训练基线，甚至在部分设置下超过有监督路由器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的本地-云协作方法通常依赖训练好的路由器或协作感知微调，将路由行为绑定到特定运行制度。论文指出这种训练可能是不必要的：本地模型在推理时跨采样响应的内在一致性已经提供了强信号，可用于决定何时信任本地执行、何时卸载到更强的云模型。

Method: 提出 CARGO，一种无训练路由框架：1) 通过提示变采样估计本地模型的响应一致性；2) 应用贝叶斯早停实现样本高效的不确定性控制；3) 通过轻量级部署时校准支持任意目标协作比例。

Result: 在多样化的推理和问答任务、多个本地 LLM 系列和规模、预训练和微调后的本地模型上，CARGO 持续优于其他无训练基线，并在若干设置中超越有监督学习的路由器。

Conclusion: 有效且自适应的本地-云协作可以直接从本地模型的内在响应行为中涌现，无需额外训练路由器。

Abstract: Local-cloud collaboration is a practical way to deploy large language models under resource constraints, but existing methods often rely on trained routers or collaboration-aware finetuning that tie routing behavior to a particular operating regime. In this work, we show that such training may be unnecessary: the local model's own inference-time agreement across sampled responses already provides a strong signal for deciding when to trust local execution and when to offload to a stronger cloud model. We propose CARGO, a training-free routing framework that estimates this agreement through prompt-varied sampling, applies Bayesian early stopping for sample-efficient uncertainty control, and supports arbitrary target collaboration ratios through lightweight deployment-time calibration. Across diverse reasoning and question-answering tasks, multiple local LLM families and scales, and both pretrained and finetuned local models, CARGO consistently outperforms other training-free baselines and in several settings surpasses supervised learned routers. These results suggest that effective and adaptable local-cloud collaboration can emerge directly from the local model's intrinsic response behavior, without requiring an additional trained router.

</details>


### [92] [PersonaTrail: Benchmarking Personalized Web Agents through Browsing Trails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20482)
*Seungbin Yang,Chaewoon Ki,Dohyun Lee,Jaegul Choo,ChaeHun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PersonaTrail 基准和 PACMem 框架，解决个性化 Web 代理在欠规范指令下从浏览历史推断用户偏好和回忆信息的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法捕捉个性化需求，要么限制任务为完全显式提示，要么将交互历史抽象为简化形式，导致代理难以从真实浏览轨迹中学习用户偏好。

Method: 1. 构建 PersonaTrail 基准，使用真实浏览轨迹作为用户历史，在托管开放 Web 环境中评估代理推断偏好和回忆信息的能力。2. 提出 PACMem 框架，将原始浏览历史分解为事实记忆（会话摘要）和偏好记忆（反复行为模式），推理时检索最相关条目指导个性化导航。

Result: 大量实验表明，PACMem 在两项任务上均持续优于现有基于记忆的基线方法。

Conclusion: PersonaTrail 填补了个性化 Web 代理评估的空白，PACMem 通过结构化记忆分解有效利用浏览历史，显著提升了代理在欠规范指令下的个性化导航性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have enabled web agents to autonomously execute complex tasks. In practice, users frequently provide underspecified instructions, requiring agents to infer the missing context from their raw browsing histories. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this form of personalization, as they either restrict tasks to fully explicit prompts or abstract web interaction history into simplified forms. To bridge this gap, we introduce PersonaTrail, a benchmark for personalized web agents operating in a managed open web environment. By leveraging realistic browsing trajectories as user history, PersonaTrail evaluates an agent's ability to infer user preferences and recall information from past browsing sessions. We further propose Preference-Aware Contextual Memory (PACMem), a framework that decomposes raw browsing histories into two types of structured memory: factual memories that summarize individual sessions and preference memories that distill recurring behavioral patterns. At inference time, the agent retrieves the most relevant entries from these memories to guide personalized navigation. Extensive experiments show that PACMem consistently outperforms existing memory-based baselines on both tasks.

</details>


### [93] [Tractable Hierarchical Control of Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20483)
*Max Scribner,Antonio Vergari,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效方法，将自回归大语言模型蒸馏为可处理的概率模型，通过掩码和引导生成过程，保证输出满足LR(k)上下文无关语法的语法约束，且将约束满足概率的计算复杂度从指数时间降低至多项式时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在程序合成等任务中，大语言模型生成的代码或数据（如SQL、JSON）必须语法合法才能被后续系统处理。现有方法将LLM生成约束为LR(k)语法时计算复杂度呈指数级，难以大规模应用，亟需更高效的约束求解方法。

Method: 将LLM蒸馏为可处理的概率模型，在自回归生成过程中引入掩码机制，结合LR(k)语法的有限时长特性，设计多项式时间算法计算满足语法约束的概率，从而引导生成过程朝向合法输出。

Result: 证明任意有限时长的LR(k)语法约束满足概率可在多项式时间内计算，显著优于先前方法的指数时间复杂度，实现了高效的LLM生成约束与引导。

Conclusion: 该成果使得大语言模型在形式化系统集成中能高效生成语法保证合法的结构化输出，为程序合成、数据生成等下游任务提供了可靠的理论与算法支撑。

Abstract: Constraining the generation of autoregressive large language models (LLMs) is an important component of integrating language models into formal systems. In the generation of code and data for tasks like program synthesis, ensuring that language models produce syntactically valid output is a prerequisite for processing such output. These languages (such as SQL or JSON) are often designed as $LR(k)$ context-free grammars. By distilling the LLM to a tractable probabilistic model, its autoregressive generation can be steered and masked to incorporate the probability of satisfying logical constraints, ensuring high quality output that is guaranteed to be valid. This paper demonstrates that the satisfaction of any $LR(k)$ grammar of finite duration can be calculated in polynomial time, an improvement over the exponential time of applying previous methods to such grammars. This result enables efficient constraint and steering of LLM generation towards output that better satisfies formal syntactic constraints.

</details>


### [94] [The Devil is in the Spectrum: Mitigating Representation Collapse in LLMs via Topologically Regularized Side-Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20484)
*Yiheng Tao,Kaiwen Cheng,Yao Lu,Chang Liu,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为拓扑正则化侧路径（TRSP）的非侵入式架构干预，通过无参数的三角框机制和轻量级长度感知门控，解决大语言模型长上下文中的表征崩溃问题，实现了谱平衡，显著提升长上下文性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在长上下文中面临表征崩溃瓶颈，现有方法易陷入同质化崩溃（注意力沉降导致秩缺失）或隔离崩溃（局部注意力导致上下文断连）两种病态极端，谱分析揭示了混合效率（谱隙）与信息容量（有效秩）的内在权衡，标准机制难以平衡。

Method: 提出拓扑正则化侧路径（TRSP），采用无参数的三角框机制配合轻量级长度感知门控，正则化token交互拓扑；通过近端耦合保持有效秩、远端传播支持非退化混合，实现几何上更健康的转移算子，且不改变核心注意力机制。

Result: 在通用能力和长上下文基准测试中显著提升；特别是在NoLiMa任务上，训练长度8倍外推时，TRSP保持83%准确率，分别比Differential Transformer和Gated Attention高出约30和50个百分点。

Conclusion: TRSP通过拓扑正则化侧路径有效解决了长上下文表征崩溃问题，实现了谱隙与有效秩的平衡，为大语言模型长上下文建模提供了非侵入式、参数高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are fundamentally limited by representation collapse, a bottleneck that severely degrades long-context performance. We identify that existing approaches risk drifting into one of two pathological extremes: homogenization collapse (e.g., attention sinks causing rank deficiency) and isolation collapse (e.g., local attention causing context disconnection). Through spectral analysis of attention dynamics, we derive an intrinsic trade-off between mixing efficiency (spectral gap) and information capacity (effective rank) that standard mechanisms struggle to balance. To resolve this dilemma, we propose the Topologically Regularized Side-Path (TRSP), a non-invasive architectural intervention that achieves spectral balance. TRSP employs a parameter-free Triangular Box mechanism, scaled by a lightweight, length-aware gate, to regularize the token interaction topology. By integrating proximal coupling to preserve effective rank and distal propagation to support non-degenerate mixing, TRSP promotes a geometrically healthier transition operator without altering core attention. Experiments show significant improvements across general capabilities and long-context benchmarks. Notably, on NoLiMa at $8\times$ the training length, TRSP retains $83\%$ accuracy and surpasses the Differential Transformer and Gated Attention by approximately 30 and 50 percentage points, respectively. Code available at: https://github.com/Eziotao-tyd/TRSP.

</details>


### [95] [Expectation Alignment of Language Models for Real-World User Expectations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20485)
*Miaomiao Li,Yang Wang,Bin Liang,Shudong Liu,Zhiwei Zhang,Kam-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统研究真实用户对大语言模型的期望，提出提取语义丰富期望的 principled 程序并构建 ExpectBench 基准，发现现有 LLMs 难以满足和预测用户期望，进而提出轻量级潜在期望感知响应生成框架 LENS，显著提升期望满足度，强调显式建模用户期望对实现真实人类-AI 对齐的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法依赖模型启发式、专家量表或用户模拟，无法捕捉真实人类期望的多样性和细微差别，导致模型在基准上表现优异却与用户实际需求错位，亟需基于真实用户期望的系统性研究和评估基准。

Method: 1. 提出 principled 程序从真实交互中提取语义丰富的用户期望；2. 构建 ExpectBench 基准，基于真实用户期望评估模型；3. 设计 LENS 框架，通过轻量级潜在期望建模使模型内化用户期望并生成更对齐的响应。

Result: 实验表明当前 LLMs 在满足和预测用户期望方面表现不佳，揭示了根本的错位来源；LENS 框架在多个设置下持续提升期望满足度，验证了显式建模用户期望的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模用户期望是实现真实人类-AI 对齐的关键，ExpectBench 和 LENS 为这一方向提供了基准和可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance on standard benchmarks, yet it remains largely unexplored whether they truly meet user expectations. Existing evaluation approaches, relying on model heuristics, expert rubrics, or user simulation, fail to capture the diversity and subtlety of real human expectations, causing models to appear competent while misaligning with what users actually seek. We present the first systematic study of user expectations in real-world LLM interactions, proposing a principled procedure to extract semantically rich expectations and introducing ExpectBench, a benchmark grounded in real user expectations. Analyses reveal that current LLMs struggle to satisfy and anticipate what users hope to obtain, highlighting a fundamental source of misalignment. Building on these observations, we propose LENS, a lightweight latent expectation-aware response generation framework. LENS enables models to internalize user expectations and generate better-aligned responses, consistently improving expectation satisfaction and underscoring the importance of explicitly modeling user expectations for realistic human-AI alignment.

</details>


### [96] [OPTScientist: Multi-Agent Discovery of Typed Optimizer Programs for Transformer Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20486)
*Zhongzheng Li,Tiancan Feng,Wenhao Li,Qingsong Ran,Shikun Feng,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yue Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPTScientist是一个基于理论指导的多智能体框架，用于在类型化领域特定语言(DSL)中自动发现优化器。它通过四个角色智能体(Theorist、Designer、Engineer、Reviewer)协作，结合进化搜索和DSL扩展机制，成功发现了RS-MR优化器，在Transformer预训练中超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习优化器设计面临优化几何、状态动力学、数值稳定性、实现约束和泛化性能的联合考量。现有自动优化器发现方法要么搜索无约束代码空间(产生无效/不可解释程序)，要么局限于狭窄参数化优化器族(限制创新性)。

Method: 提出OPTScientist框架：将优化器设计形式化为受约束的科学搜索过程，候选更新通过方向、缩放、预条件、正则化、状态和分组模块表达。四个角色智能体在单一编排循环中协作：Theorist提出假设，Designer合成DSL候选，Engineer编译评估，Reviewer评判结果。结合优化器程序的进化搜索与二阶段DSL扩展机制，当重复失败揭示表达瓶颈时提出小规模DSL扩展。

Result: 发现RS-MR——一种降维状态矩阵优化器，在原生评估协议下的Transformer预训练任务中超越强基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明了一条基于理论、类型化程序、编译器验证和闭环实验的自动化优化器科学路径。

Abstract: Designing optimizers for modern deep learning remains a challenging scientific problem, requiring the joint consideration of optimization geometry, state dynamics, numerical stability, implementation constraints, and empirical generalization. Existing automated optimizer discovery methods typically search either over unconstrained code spaces or within narrowly parameterized optimizer families. The former is flexible but often produces invalid or uninterpretable programs, while the latter is stable but limits novelty. We introduce OPTScientist, a theory-guided multi-agent framework for optimizer discovery in a typed domain-specific language (DSL). OPTScientist formulates optimizer design as a constrained scientific search process, where candidate updates are expressed through direction, scaling, preconditioning, regularization, state, and grouping modules. Four role agents, Theorist, Designer, Engineer, and Reviewer, collaborate within a single orchestration loop to propose hypotheses, synthesize DSL candidates, compile and evaluate optimizers, and critique results. To overcome the limitations of a fixed search space, OPTScientist combines evolutionary search over optimizer programs with a second-stage mechanism that proposes small DSL extensions when repeated failures reveal representational bottlenecks. Using this framework, we discover RS-MR, a reduced-state matrix optimizer that improves transformer pretraining over strong baselines under our native evaluation protocol. Our results suggest a path toward automated optimizer science grounded in theory, typed programs, compiler validation, and closed-loop experimentation.

</details>


### [97] [Directional Hallucinations: Ideological Drift in News-Grounded LLM Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20487)
*Chendi Wang,Liam Cunningham,Tom Yishay,Jieying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复现的测量框架，将文档 grounding 问答中的幻觉作为意识形态漂移的诊断信号，利用 21,727 篇专家标注的美国政治新闻文章，评估四个大语言模型在选举相关场景下的幻觉率与意识形态偏向。结果显示：幻觉率随模型和争议话题显著变化，但源意识形态对幻觉频率影响较小；幻觉内容呈现稳健的左倾漂移，即使源自右倾来源；logit 分析揭示幻觉源于高熵生成语境，不确定性在某些模型中预测左倾漂移，符合“不确定性到猜测”机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多用于回答政治信息问题，尤其在选举临近的高风险场景中，事实错误和意识形态扭曲后果严重。现有研究缺乏系统框架量化文档 grounding QA 中的幻觉并将其与意识形态漂移关联，因此需要可复现的测量方法来审计 AI 中介的政治信息。

Method: 1. 使用 QBias 数据集（21,727 篇跨左右中立场的专家标注美国政治新闻）。2. 为每篇文章生成特定问题。3. 从三个开放权重 LLM 和一个专有模型获取文档 grounding 回答。4. 基于参考文档进行句级幻觉检测。5. 用微调立场分类器给幻觉句子打意识形态标签。6. 探测输出 logits，关联 token 级不确定性与幻觉及漂移。

Result: 幻觉率在模型间差异大，集中于争议话题；来源意识形态对幻觉频率影响适中。幻觉内容表现出强左倾漂移：多数幻觉句子被判为左倾，甚至源自右倾来源。Logit 分析显示幻觉发生于高熵生成语境，部分模型中不确定性也预测左倾漂移，支持“不确定性到猜测”机制。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为审计 AI 中介政治信息的工具，揭示幻觉不仅是事实错误，更承载系统性意识形态偏向。为选举相关部署设计防护措施提供实证依据，建议结合不确定性感知解码与意识形态平衡微调来缓解左倾漂移风险。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to answer questions about political information, including in election-adjacent information settings where factual errors and ideological distortions are high-stakes. We present a reproducible measurement framework that treats hallucinations, unsupported statements in document-grounded QA, as diagnostic signals of ideological drift. Using 21,727 expert-labeled U.S. political news articles from QBias spanning left, center, and right sources, we (i) generate an article-specific question, (ii) elicit document-grounded answers from three open-weight LLMs and one proprietary model, (iii) detect sentence-level hallucinations via reference-based comparison, (iv) classify the ideological valence of hallucinated sentences with a fine-tuned stance classifier, and (v) probe output logits to relate token-level uncertainty to hallucination and drift. Hallucination rates vary substantially across models and concentrate in contentious topics, while source-ideology differences in hallucination frequency are modest. In contrast, hallucination content exhibits robust leftward drift: a majority of hallucinated sentences are classified as left-leaning, including among hallucinations generated from right-leaning sources. Logit-level analysis shows hallucinations arise in high-entropy generation contexts, and in some models uncertainty also predicts leftward drift, consistent with an "uncertainty to guessing" mechanism. We discuss implications for auditing AI-mediated political information and for designing safeguards in election-relevant deployments.

</details>


### [98] [Autonomous Topology Mutation: Safe Runtime Restructuring for Multi-Agent LLM Systems with Capability, State, and Shadow Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20488)
*Bronislav Sidik,Chaya Levi,Nizzan Kimhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出自主拓扑变异（ATM）机制，解决多智能体LLM框架运行时拓扑固定导致的过载问题，通过六信号瓶颈指数监控和三大安全不变量，实现过载智能体的动态拆分与热替换，在代码任务上将成功率从3.3%提升至61.7%，且隐私内存暴露降为零，延迟开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体LLM框架在启动时固定团队拓扑，运行时缺乏重组机制。当单个智能体因动作类别过多、工具错误累积或调用排队而过载时，系统无法自我重构，导致性能下降甚至失败。

Method: 引入自主拓扑变异（ATM）运行时团队变异机制：1) 六信号瓶颈指数监控（队列深度、上下文抖动、工具错误率、角色熵、重试循环率、跨智能体等待时间）；2) 三大安全不变量门控结构变更：能力单调性、状态路由完整性、影子验证后上线；3) 过载时将智能体因式分解为专用子智能体，父智能体热切换为协调器角色；4) 隐私级别感知的状态路由传输；5) 影子验证窗口确保候选拓扑通过后才接收实时流量。

Result: 在720次DeepSeek-V3驱动的任务运行中：ATM因式分解使代码任务成功率从3.3%提升至61.7%；完整轨道与蒸馏系统将高隐私内存暴露从每任务2.0次降至0.0次，同时保持任务质量；运行时轨道在代理热路径上增加的p99延迟小于500微秒；包含真实Python执行的小型实时工具探针作为外部有效性检查。实现、基准测试套件和追踪已开源。

Conclusion: ATM通过遥测驱动的过载检测和安全不变量保障，实现了多智能体LLM系统的运行时动态拓扑重组，显著提升了代码任务成功率并消除了高隐私内存泄露，且引入的延迟开销微乎其微，为自适应多智能体系统提供了实用且安全的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM frameworks typically fix their team topology at boot time. When an individual agent becomes overloaded at runtime, for example by mixing too many action categories, accumulating tool errors, or queueing behind too many calls, the system has no mechanism to restructure itself. We introduce Autonomous Topology Mutation (ATM), a runtime team-mutation mechanism for multi-agent LLM frameworks. ATM combines telemetry-driven overload detection with three safety invariants that gate each structural change: capability monotonicity, state-routing completeness, and shadow-before-live validation.
  ATM monitors a six-signal Bottleneck Index that includes queue depth, context thrash, tool-error rate, role entropy, retry-loop rate, and cross-agent wait time. When a warmup-calibrated threshold is breached for multiple consecutive ticks, ATM factorises the overloaded agent into specialised sub-agents and hot-swaps the parent into a coordinator role while preserving its external identity. State transfer is controlled by privacy-level-aware routing: each memory atom is routed only to a permitted child set, or explicitly dropped with a logged reason. No candidate topology receives live traffic until it has passed a shadow validation window.
  On 720 DeepSeek-V3-driven task runs with deterministic tool stubs across four ablation conditions and three workloads, the ATM factoriser split lifts code-task success from 3.3% to 61.7%. The full rail-and-distillation system reduces detected high-privacy memory exposure under a regex classifier from 2.0 to 0.0 events per task while preserving task quality. The runtime rails carrying ATM's invariants add less than 500 microseconds of p99 latency on the agent hot path. A small live-tool probe with real Python execution is included as an external-validity check. The implementation, benchmark harness, and traces are open-sourced.

</details>


### [99] [EvoSQL: Memory-Augmented Critic-Generator Co-Evolution for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20489)
*Jiawei Zhou,Jianwei Wang,Chenyu Zhou,Chaojian Shi,Ming Dong,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoSQL是一个生成器-评论家协同进化框架，通过迭代交互、候选记忆、执行验证和自我蒸馏策略优化，显著提升开源大模型在Text-to-SQL任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂数据库查询需要超越单次生成的推理，包括多步分解、基于执行的诊断和针对性修正，现有方法在处理复杂SQL时表现不足。

Method: 提出EvoSQL协同进化框架，包含生成器和评论家的迭代交互，维护上下文化候选记忆，利用执行信号和LLM评论验证候选SQL，通过效用引导聚合更新记忆；引入自我蒸馏策略优化（SDPO）微调阶段，将执行感知监督注入编码LLM主干。

Result: 在Spider和BIRD基准上，EvoSQL持续提升开源模型超越Maj@16基线，BIRD-Dev上增益从Qwen3-4B的+1.37%到Qwen2.5-Coder-3B的+9.19%；SDPO初始化进一步提升Spider-Test和BIRD-Dev性能。

Conclusion: 记忆驱动的协同进化是构建更可靠、泛化性更强的Text-to-SQL系统的有效路径。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL has advanced rapidly with large language models, but complex database queries still require reasoning beyond one-shot generation, including multi-step decomposition, execution-based diagnosis, and targeted correction. We present EvoSQL, a co-evolution framework that formulates SQL synthesis as an iterative interaction between a generator and a critic. EvoSQL maintains a contextualized candidate memory, verifies SQL candidates with both execution signals and LLM-based critique, and updates its memory through utility-guided aggregation. To strengthen the underlying generator-critic pair, we further introduce a Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (SDPO) fine-tuning stage that injects execution-aware supervision into modern coding LLM backbones. Experiments on Spider and BIRD show that EvoSQL consistently improves open-source models over Maj@16 baselines, with particularly large gains on BIRD-Dev, ranging from +1.37% for Qwen3-4B to +9.19% for Qwen2.5-Coder-3B. SDPO initialization further improves selected backbones on Spider-Test and BIRD-Dev. These results suggest that memory-grounded co-evolution is an effective path toward more reliable and generalizable Text-to-SQL systems. Code is available at https://github.com/valleysprings/EvoSQL.

</details>


### [100] [CRAWO: Custom Resources for Adaptive Workload Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20490)
*Eugênio Santos,Daniel Maia,Stefano Loss,José Manoel Silva,Aluizio Rocha Neto,Thais Batista,Everton Cavalcante,Nélio Cacho,Eduardo Nogueira,Daniel Araújo,Frederico Lopes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CRAWO，一种面向分布式边缘环境的 AI 流水线自适应编排框架，基于控制回路模型，结合硬件感知分配器与可插拔多准则决策层，在 K3s 上利用 CRD 与 Operator 实现，实验表明其能改善负载分布并降低对云端的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能虽能降低延迟和带宽，但异构边缘设备能力差异大，现有编排平台侧重部署自动化与基础设施管理，难以在动态条件下自适应分配资源，导致 AI 流水线部署效率低。

Method: 设计 CRAWO 架构框架：采用控制回路模型，将分配智能与执行分离，管理放置决策、状态管理和阶段间数据流；引入硬件感知分配器及可插拔多准则决策层，利用实时基础设施指标自适应放置工作负载；参考实现基于微服务架构部署于轻量级 Kubernetes (K3s)，使用自定义资源定义 (CRD) 进行领域建模，并通过专用 Operator 实现状态协调。

Result: 在车辆监控场景（车牌识别）评估中，CRAWO 实现了更优的工作负载分布，并在对延迟敏感的环境中显著减少了对中心化云端处理的依赖。

Conclusion: CRAWO 通过控制回路与硬件感知的多准则决策，有效解决了异构边缘环境下 AI 流水线的自适应编排问题，为延迟敏感型智慧城市应用提供了可落地的边缘智能编排方案。

Abstract: Edge Intelligence has emerged as a key paradigm for enabling real-time applications in smart cities by shifting computation from centralized cloud data centers to the network edge, thereby reducing latency and bandwidth consumption. However, deploying Artificial Intelligence (AI) pipelines across heterogeneous edge infrastructures remains challenging due to the wide range of device capabilities, from low-power microcontrollers to accelerator-equipped systems. Existing edge orchestration platforms primarily focus on deployment automation and infrastructure management, but these approaches are often inefficient and limit the ability to adaptively allocate resources under dynamic conditions. To tackle these issues, this paper introduces CRAWO (Custom Resources for Adaptive Workload Orchestration), an architectural framework for coordinating AI pipelines across distributed edge environments. CRAWO follows a control-loop-based model that separates allocation intelligence from execution by managing placement decisions, state management, and inter-stage data flows while instantiating services on edge nodes. The framework incorporates a hardware-aware allocator with a pluggable multi-criteria decision layer that leverages real-time infrastructure metrics to enable adaptive workload placement. The reference implementation adopts a microservices architecture deployed on a lightweight Kubernetes distribution (K3s), using Custom Resource Definitions (CRDs) for domain modeling and a dedicated operator for state reconciliation. Evaluation in a vehicle surveillance scenario using license plate recognition demonstrates improved workload distribution and reduced reliance on centralized cloud processing in latency-sensitive environments.

</details>


### [101] [DFAH-Bench: Benchmarking Observable Agent Instability in Financial Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20491)
*Raffi Khatchadourian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了 DFAH-Bench，一个用于评估金融智能体决策行为稳定性的重放基准，通过工具调用轨迹、证据接触和决策集中度三个可观测通道，无需隐藏推理文本即可量化行为不稳定性。实验表明，仅看结果一致性不足以反映过程稳定性：前沿模型决策一致率达 95%，但工具路径一致率仅 77%，存在 18 个百分点的差距；高一致性群体中超 55% 表现出显著轨迹分歧。识别出三类行为画像：模式匹配器、稳定执行器和轨迹分歧者。基准代码、指标脚本、重放日志等已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估基准仅关注工具使用智能体的决策结果，忽略了决策过程的稳定性。作者旨在填补这一空白，提出一种无需访问隐藏推理文本、仅基于可观测行为（工具调用轨迹、证据接触、决策集中度）即可量化行为不稳定性的重放基准。

Method: 构建 DFAH-Bench 基准，包含三个金融任务，对 10 个模型进行 8,127 次重放实验。通过三个通道测量行为稳定性：工具调用轨迹、证据接触、决策集中度。对比结果一致性与过程一致性，识别行为画像。

Result: 前沿模型决策一致率 95%，但工具路径一致率仅 77%，差距 18 个百分点（95% CI: [0.14, 0.22]）。高决策一致性群体中超 55% 存在显著轨迹分歧。识别出三类行为画像：模式匹配器（无论输入输出单一结果）、稳定执行器（工具使用过程相对一致）、轨迹分歧者（通过不同工具路径和证据得出相同结论）。

Conclusion: 仅评估结果一致性不足以保证智能体行为的可靠性，过程层面的稳定性评估至关重要。DFAH-Bench 为金融智能体的行为稳定性提供了可复现、可观测的评估框架，揭示了当前模型在决策过程上的隐性不稳定性，为后续鲁棒性改进提供了依据。

Abstract: Standard evaluation benchmarks measure what a tool-using agent decides, not whether it arrives at that decision through the same process each time. We introduce DFAH-Bench, a replay benchmark that measures observable behavioral instability in financial agent decision-making across three channels -- tool-call trajectories, evidence contacts, and decision concentration -- none of which require access to hidden reasoning text. Across 8,127 replay episodes spanning 10 models and 3 financial tasks, we find that outcome agreement alone is an incomplete stability signal: frontier models can agree on decisions 95% of the time while following the same tool path only 77% of the time -- an 18-percentage-point gap (95% CI: [0.14, 0.22]) that outcome-only evaluation misses entirely. Among frontier-model case groups with high decision agreement, over 55% exhibit meaningful trajectory divergence. We identify three behavioral profiles: pattern matchers that achieve near-perfect agreement by collapsing to a single output regardless of input, stable executors with relatively consistent tool-use processes, and trajectory divergers that reach the same conclusions through materially different tool paths and evidence contacts. The benchmark code, metric scripts, replay logs, benchmark card, dataset README, and release manifest are released in the accompanying repository.

</details>


### [102] [Attention-based Experience Replay Framework for Continual Learning of Agnostic Time Series Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20493)
*Quentin Besnard,Nicolas Ragot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力机制引导的经验回放策略的持续时间序列预测框架，旨在解决神经网络对大型静态数据集的依赖及灾难性遗忘问题，实现在动态环境下的增量学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在机器人、成像和声音处理等领域取得显著进展，但神经网络严重依赖大型静态数据集，而现实应用中数据分布随时间变化，环境动态演化，难以满足静态数据集条件。持续学习旨在开发能在计算约束下平衡稳定性和可塑性的增量适应模型。

Method: 提出一种新的持续时间序列预测框架，扩展现有静态预测模型，结合由注意力机制引导的经验回放策略，使模型能动态适应新上下文同时保留先验知识，有效缓解灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在标准预测基准和表现出多样化时间行为的压力计数据集上评估，结果显示该方法能随时间有效提升或保持预测性能，同时降低再训练成本和数据需求。

Conclusion: 该框架促进了预测模型在动态和真实世界场景中的部署，为解决时间序列预测中的持续学习挑战提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Deep learning has led to remarkable progress in artificial intelligence, particularly in robotics, imaging and sound processing. However, a major limitation of neural networks remains their strong dependence on large and stationary datasets. In many real-world applications, these conditions are rarely met due to evolving and dynamic environments where data distributions change over time. Continual learning aims to address this challenge by developing models capable of adapting incrementally while maintaining a balance between stability and plasticity under computational constraints. In this work, we introduce a novel framework for continual time series forecasting, designed to extend existing static forecasting models commonly used in the literature by incorporating an Experience Replay strategy guided by Attention mechanisms. This approach allows the model to adapt dynamically to new contexts while preserving prior knowledge, effectively mitigating catastrophic forgetting. The framework is evaluated on standard forecasting benchmarks as well as on a piezometric dataset exhibiting diverse temporal behaviors. Results show that our approach effectively increases or maintains predictive performance over time while reducing retraining costs and data requirements, thus facilitating the deployment of forecasting models in dynamic and real-world settings.

</details>


### [103] [Isolating LLM Alignment from Regex: Zero Coverage and Metric-Dependent Divergence Under Adversarial Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20494)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究正则过滤器与模型侧对齐在生产 LLM 应用中的协同效果，发现当语料库设计为绕过正则时，纯对齐模块在子串匹配指标上零拦截，但在 LLM 评判指标下能检测到 56–100% 的拒答，揭示对齐贡献依赖于评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作发现，在激活的正则过滤器后添加实时 Gemini 后端无法带来可测量的覆盖增益；本文旨在验证当语料库专门设计为绕过正则时，该性能上限是否依然成立。

Method: 构建 L5-no-regex（即 L4-real 但禁用九条正则模式），针对三个子语料（carry-forward、regex-bypass、alignment-isolate）的 45 个对抗探针，经 Gemini 改写与 PAIR 扩增至约 1,555 对探针-运行对，跨 5 次复现实验；使用主指标子串分类器与次指标 LLM 评判器评估拦截率。

Result: 主指标下 L5 拦截率 0%（Δpp=0 vs L0, p=1.00，Wilson 上界 <5%），H1 被证伪；次指标下 PAIR 变体拦截率 56–100%（p<0.01），显示对齐对对抗性探针有响应但拒答过于细微无法被子串匹配捕捉；子语料差异预测不成立（p=1.00）。

Conclusion: 对齐的贡献取决于评估指标：在自然语言有害请求探针上，对齐在正则之上零覆盖增益；在对抗性框架变体上，LLM 评判器能检测到子串分类器遗漏的拒答。相关语料、变异工件与导出脚本已开源以便复现。

Abstract: Production LLM applications commonly stack a regex filter in front of model-side alignment; prior work found no measurable coverage gain from adding a live Gemini backend behind an active regex filter. We ask whether that ceiling holds when the corpus is \emph{designed to bypass the regex}. We introduce $L_5$-no-regex -- identical to $L_4$-real (Gemini-2.5-flash, token-budget cap, rate limit, output scrub) but with the nine-pattern filter disabled -- and evaluate it against $N{=}45$ adversarial probes across three sub-corpora (carry-forward, regex-bypass, alignment-isolate), amplified by Gemini paraphrase and PAIR to ${\sim}1{,}555$ probe-run pairs over $N{=}5$ replications. Under the primary substring classifier, H1 is refuted: $L_5$ block rate is $0,%$ across all five OWASP LLM Top-10 categories ($Δ\text{pp}{=}0$ vs.\ $L_0$, $p{=}1.00$; Wilson upper bound ${<}5,%$). A secondary LLM-judge metric on PAIR variants shows $56$--$100,%$ block rates ($p{<}0.01$), revealing alignment does respond to adversarially-framed probes -- but produces refusals too nuanced for substring matching. The sub-corpus differential prediction is not supported ($p{=}1.00$). Alignment's contribution is \emph{metric-dependent}: on natural-language harmful-request probes, it adds zero observed coverage beyond the regex; on adversarially-framed variants, an LLM judge detects refusals the substring classifier misses. The locked corpus, mutation artifacts, and export scripts are released for replication.

</details>


### [104] [Workload-Aware Caching for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20495)
*Anas Mohamed,Kaizan Haque,Azal Ahmad Khan,Chetan Sharma,Shuwen Ge,Ali Anwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种面向多智能体系统的负载感知缓存淘汰策略，结合重新计算成本、DAG依赖计数、智能体调用频率三个信号设计统一评分函数，在有限内存下保留最高价值的中间结果。在三个基准测试中，该策略相较无缓存基线最高降低64.7%延迟，平均比次优有限容量基线降低31.1%延迟，性能接近无界缓存且准确率不降反增，且与计划级缓存、并行执行等优化互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统将复杂任务分解为专用智能体执行的有向无环图（DAG），天然产生跨查询复用中间结果的机会。现有缓存淘汰策略仅基于访问历史统一处理所有条目，忽略了智能体执行环境特有的结构信号（如依赖拓扑）和工作负载信号（如重计算开销、调用频率），导致缓存价值评估不准确。

Method: 设计工作负载感知淘汰策略，融合三个信号：1) 重新计算成本——缓存命中避免的计算开销；2) DAG依赖计数——该节点作为下游任务前置条件的依赖数量；3) 智能体调用频率——该智能体在历史工作负载中被调用的频次。将三者归一化后加权求和形成统一评分函数，淘汰时优先保留评分最高的条目。

Result: 在三个多智能体基准（覆盖不同复用模式）上评估：相较无缓存基线延迟最高降低64.7%；平均比最优有限容量基线（LRU/LFU等）再降低31.1%延迟；性能逼近无界缓存上界；准确率与所有有限容量竞争方法持平或更高。消融实验验证三信号均不可或缺。进一步证明该策略与计划级缓存、并行智能体执行等优化正交互补，分别针对管道中不同效率瓶颈。

Conclusion: 工作负载感知的缓存淘汰策略能有效利用多智能体系统特有的结构与负载信号，在内存受限条件下最大化缓存价值，显著降低端到端延迟且不损准确率。该方法为多智能体系统的缓存层设计提供了新范式，且与现有优化技术正交可组合。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems decompose complex tasks into directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) of specialized agent executions, creating natural opportunities for caching intermediate results across queries. However, existing cache eviction policies treat all cached entries uniformly based on access history, ignoring structural and workload signals uniquely available in agentic execution environments. We present a workload-aware eviction policy that combines three signals, namely recomputation cost, DAG dependency count, and agent invocation frequency, into a unified scoring function that retains the most valuable entries under memory constraints. Evaluated across three multi-agent benchmarks spanning diverse reuse regimes, our policy reduces latency by up to 64.7% relative to the uncached baseline and achieves on average a 31.1% latency reduction over the next best finite-capacity baseline, while approaching the performance of an unbounded cache and maintaining accuracy on par with or exceeding all competing finite-capacity methods. We further show that workload-aware content caching is complementary to other agentic system optimization methods, including plan-level caching and parallel agent execution, with each technique targeting a distinct efficiency bottleneck in multi-agent pipelines.

</details>


### [105] [From Errors to Rules: Iterative Prompt Optimization for Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20497)
*Yueying Cui,Renhao Xue,Yi Zhang,Mukul Prasad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文对文本分类提示优化的三大范式进行了系统实证研究，发现各范式在不同任务结构上各有优势，无单一方法全胜。据此提出错误引导优化（ERGO），通过诊断-处方-重写循环迭代优化提示，在边界可学习任务上达到最优精度，并给出了范式互补选择框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示优化方法（示例选择、探索搜索、错误驱动诊断）各有优劣，但缺乏系统性对比和任务适配指导，需要明确不同范式在何种任务结构下表现最佳，并开发互补方法。

Method: 1) 在 2-150 类别的多个基准上定量和定性对比三大优化范式；2) 提出 ERGO：将训练集分为不重叠批次，迭代诊断分类错误，生成针对性决策规则，经诊断-处方-重写反馈循环更新提示。

Result: ERGO 在错误集中于特定混淆标签对的边界可学习任务上表现最优：TREC 90.0%，CLINC150 94.4%，3-5 轮收敛，生成可解释规则。三大范式互补：示例型 ICL 适合覆盖依赖任务，探索搜索适合多类别意图，ERGO 适合决策边界可从错误模式学习的任务。

Conclusion: 论文建立了任务特征到最优范式选择的互补框架，为实践者提供了根据任务结构选择提示优化策略的实用指南，并证明了错误驱动方法在边界可学习任务上的独特价值。

Abstract: Prompt optimization for text classification spans diverse approaches, from demonstration selection to exploration-based search to error-driven diagnosis, each with known but incompletely characterized strengths and limitations. We conduct a comprehensive empirical study across diverse classification benchmarks (2 to 150 classes) comparing these paradigms through both quantitative evaluation and qualitative analysis of optimization traces, revealing that each paradigm excels on structurally different task types and that no single method dominates. Guided by these insights, we propose Error-Guided Optimization (ERGO), an error-driven method that iterates over the full training set in non-overlapping batches, diagnoses classification failures, and generates targeted decision rules through a diagnose-prescribe-rewrite feedback loop. ERGO achieves the best accuracy on tasks where errors concentrate in specific confused label pairs (which we term boundary-learnable tasks): TREC: 90.0%, CLINC150: 94.4%, converges in 3-5 iterations, and produces interpretable decision rules. While ERGO does not achieve the highest overall average, it fills a complementary role: demonstration-based ICL wins on coverage-dependent tasks, exploration-based search wins on many-class intent, and ERGO wins where decision boundaries are learnable from error patterns. We provide a complementarity framework linking task characteristics to optimal paradigm selection, offering practical guidance for practitioners.

</details>


### [106] [AISE-Bench: A Full-Cycle Curated Benchmark for Information Seeking on Academic Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20498)
*Fanjin Zhang,Zhengyang Wang,Ruixuan Huang,Kefan Zhang,Amy Xin,Yuanchun Wang,Shu Zhao,Evgeny Kharlamov,Jie Tang,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 AISE-Bench，一个真实世界的全周期标注基准，用于评估大语言模型在学术知识图谱上进行信息检索的工具使用能力。基准包含 1133 个 QA 对，涵盖查询分类、完整 API 执行轨迹、验证参数及带引用链接的答案。作者设计了定制化标注工作流，并开发了综合评估协议，测试了 14 种方法，发现最强模型（PLAY2PROMPT + Gemini-3-Pro）仅达中等水平，且在 API 规划和执行上仍有困难。AISE-Bench 为多步骤 API 调用 LLM 代理的逐步正确性、有据可查的摘要和可追踪推理提供了具有挑战性的测试床。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学术图谱信息检索工具使用基准多依赖合成模板、简化解空间或狭窄的以论文为中心的任务，未充分探索真实用户意图、复杂多步 API 规划、丰富参数填充、带引用的落地答案以及对过程和结果的全面评估等关键挑战。

Method: 构建 AISE-Bench：收集 1133 个真实 QA 对，标注查询分类、完整 API 执行轨迹、验证参数及带引用链接的答案。设计定制化代理工作流辅助标注员高效规划、执行和修订复杂 API 工作流。开发综合评估协议，从答案质量、引用落地、API 规划正确性、执行成功率四个维度量化评估。在 14 种方法（含 PLAY2PROMPT + Gemini-3-Pro 等）上进行实验。

Result: 最强模型（PLAY2PROMPT + Gemini-3-Pro）仅达到中等性能，常在 API 规划和执行环节出错。多数方法在多步 API 规划、参数填充及答案引用落地方面表现不足。基准揭示了当前 LLM 代理在复杂学术知识图谱检索任务中的系统性短板。

Conclusion: AISE-Bench 确立了一个具有挑战性的新测试床，可定量评估和改进多步 API 调用 LLM 代理的逐步正确性、有据可查的摘要生成和可追踪推理能力。代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) augmented with tools are emerging as autonomous agents capable of using Web engine, APIs, and code to solve complex, long-horizon tasks. Current tool-using benchmarks for information seeking on academic graphs rely on synthetic templates, simplified solution spaces, or narrow tasks such as paper-centric tasks, leaving key challenges underexplored - realistic user intent, complex multi-step API planning, rich parameter filling for APIs, grounded answers with references, and comprehensive evaluation of both the process and the outcome. We introduce AISE-Bench, a real-world, full-cycle annotated benchmark for information seeking on academic knowledge graphs. AISE-Bench release contains 1,133 QA pairs, including query taxonomies, full API execution trajectories, validated parameters, and source-grounded answers with reference links. To support high-quality annotation, we design a customized agent workflow to enable annotators to plan, execute, and revise complex API workflows efficiently. We develop a comprehensive evaluation protocol measuring answer quality, reference grounding, API-planning correctness, and execution success. Among the 14 evaluated methods, even the strongest model (PLAY2PROMPT with Gemini-3-Pro) achieves only moderate performance and often struggles with API planning and execution. AISE-Bench establishes a challenging new testbed for quantitatively evaluating and improving the stepwise correctness, grounded summarization, and traceable reasoning of multi-step API-using LLM agents. Our code and data are available at https://aise-bench.github.io/.

</details>


### [107] [ExecuGraph: A Multi-Agent, Execution-Grounded Framework for Reliable Backend Code Synthesis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20499)
*Sai Deekshith Lekkala,Jothi Prabha Appadurai,Rohith Reddy Bellibatlu,Manpreet Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ExecuGraph是一个以执行验证为核心的多智能体框架，用于后端代码合成，通过六个专业化智能体协作和有界重试预算，在HumanEval和跨模型实验中显示出优势，尤其在强基座模型上多智能体分解价值显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的后端代码虽看似合理，但单次生成无法保证正确性和运行时可靠性，需要引入执行验证机制和多智能体协作来提升代码合成质量。

Method: 提出ExecuGraph框架，包含六个专业化智能体（规划器、代码生成器、逻辑审查器、评估器、优化器、解释器），通过有类型的有向工作流和有界重试预算协作，基于LangGraph实现，使用本地托管模型（Ollama）和可选检索层，配备子进程隔离沙箱和壁钟超时保护每次评估。

Result: 在internal-30基准上三种条件统计无显著差异（p=0.59和0.08）；HumanEval上多智能体完整版领先+3.1个百分点；跨模型实验显示DeepSeekCoder V2 Lite下图类别准确率从57.5%提升至80.0%（+22.5pp），支持多智能体分解价值随基座模型能力增强的扩展假设。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是方法论层面：单一代码库通过配置可折叠为单次生成、执行重试和逐智能体消融条件，实现对各杠杆边际贡献的受控测量，并报告了逐智能体消融、重试预算扫描、错误类别分类学和测试来源审计。

Abstract: Large Language Models generate plausible backend code, but a single-pass paradigm provides no guarantee of correctness or runtime reliability. We present ExecuGraph, a multi-agent framework that places execution-based validation at the center of backend code synthesis. Six specialized agents (Planner, Code Generator, Logical Reviewer, Evaluator, Optimizer, and Explainer) are coordinated by a typed directed workflow with a bounded retry budget, implemented on LangGraph with locally hosted models (Ollama) and an optional retrieval layer for algorithmic technique recall. A subprocess-isolated sandbox with a wall-clock timeout guards every evaluation. We evaluate on a curated 30-problem DSA suite (internal-30), HumanEval (n=64), and an APPS-introductory subset, contrasting ExecuGraph against a single-agent one-shot baseline and a single-agent execution-retry baseline (a Reflexion-style ablation that isolates the contribution of multi-agent decomposition). On internal-30, the three conditions are statistically indistinguishable (n=30; paired Wilcoxon p=0.59 MF vs. SO, p=0.08 SR vs. SO); 95% bootstrap confidence intervals on all pairwise mean differences include zero. On HumanEval, multi-full edges ahead by +3.1 pp. The strongest signal is cross-model: with DeepSeekCoder V2 Lite, graph-category accuracy improves from 57.5% (oneshot) to 80.0% (multi-full), a +22.5 pp jump that supports a scaling hypothesis: the value of multi-agent decomposition grows with base-model capability. The framework's primary contribution is methodological: a single codebase that collapses by configuration into one-shot, execution-retry, and per-agent ablation conditions, enabling controlled measurement of each lever's marginal contribution. A per-agent ablation, retry-budget sweep, error-class taxonomy, and test-source audit are reported.

</details>


### [108] [FlowEdit: Information-Theoretic Control of LLM Reasoning Flows for Ill-posed Problems Involving Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20500)
*Sizhe Tang,Guangyu Jiang,Yu Li,Rongqian Chen,Ioannis G. Kevrekidis,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowEdit框架，利用信息论原理量化并调节大语言模型（LLM）内部推理流，使其能针对含有冲突、不一致条件的 ill-posed 问题，在一次前向传播中生成多个互斥假设下的完整替代响应集合。实验显示，FlowEdit 在精确集合匹配准确率上超越领先专有模型 68%，整体响应信息量提升 24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的问题常因条件不一致、陈述冲突或需求互斥而变为 ill-posed，无单一有效答案。现有 LLM 受限于逐 token 预测机制，难以显性化隐性冲突、维护多路假设分支并一次性给出多样化替代响应。因此需要新方法在模型内部显式建模并调控多路推理流。

Method: FlowEdit 引入两个对偶信息论目标作用于模型内部推理表示：1) 最大化从每个选定假设到对应分支输出的信息流（条件互信息）；2) 最小化兄弟分支间的重叠与条件依赖。通过在 ε-充分边界嵌入下的可解变分界优化底层条件互信息，实现分支感知的推理过程，产出覆盖广、多样性强的响应集合。

Result: 大量实验表明：FlowEdit 在精确集合匹配指标上比最强专有基线高出 68%，整体响应信息量（informativeness）提升 24%。进一步分析显示，流调控表现为 token 流中下一 token 熵的重新分布：熵集中在各分支内部、在流边界处放大，并随问题所需流数缩放。

Conclusion: FlowEdit 通过信息论驱动的分支感知推理机制，有效解决了 LLM 在 ill-posed 冲突问题上的多假设生成难题，显著提升了响应集合的准确性与信息覆盖度，为开放世界复杂推理提供了可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly on well-specified reasoning tasks with a feasible answer. However, problems encountered in the open world can become ill-posed due to inconsistent conditions, conflicting statements, or mutually incompatible requirements, admitting no valid responses. We argue that reasoning of such ill-posed problems involving conflicts require novel LLM capabilities to make hidden conflicts explicit, maintain competing hypotheses via multiple reasoning branches, and generate alternative responses in a single pass, all of which are challenging due to the limitation of the next-token prediction mechanism in LLMs. To this end, we propose FlowEdit, a novel framework that leverages information-theoretic principles to quantify and regulate internal reasoning flows of LLMs, for generating a full set of alternative responses under valid hypotheses. FlowEdit can be viewed as enforcing a branch-aware reasoning process using two dual information-theoretic objectives on the model's internal reasoning representations: maximizing the information flow from each selected hypothesis to the branch outcome, while minimizing the overlap and conditional dependence across sibling branches, to provide a diverse, informative set of responses with broad coverage. We show that this is achieved through tractable variational bounds under boundary embeddings being ε-sufficient, optimizing the underlying conditional mutual information in LLM reasoning process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FlowEdit outperforms leading proprietary models, improving exact-set-match accuracy by 68%, while boosting overall response informativeness by 24%. We further show that flow regulation surfaces in the token stream as a redistribution of next-token entropy that concentrates inside each branch, amplifies at flow boundaries, and scales with the number of flows the problem requires.

</details>


### [109] [MKEvolve: A Modular Multi-Agent Framework for Kernel Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20501)
*Jason Yoo,Rajarshi Saha,Shaowei Zhu,Tao Yu,Wei Tang,Youngsuk Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MKEvolve 是一个迭代协同进化框架，将复杂 PyTorch 模块分解为子模块，并为每个子模块生成内核，通过分裂与融合优化分解，利用 LLM 驱动的束搜索独立改进每个子内核，实现了可配置、可解释且可复用的内核组合。在 Triton 和 KernelBench L2/L3 上实验显示，其正确性与加速比均优于端到端直接合成基线，且 LLM token 使用量降低高达 35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于 LLM 的代码生成快速发展，但为硬件加速器编写正确且高性能的内核仍是扩展现代 ML 工作负载的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出 MKEvolve 框架，迭代地协同进化复杂 PyTorch 模块的模块化分解与各子模块的 LLM 生成内核，通过分裂与融合在迭代中细化分解，并利用 LLM 驱动的束搜索独立优化每个子内核。

Result: 在 Triton 和 KernelBench L2/L3（涵盖多算子序列和完整模型架构）上的实验表明，MKEvolve 在正确性和加速比上均超越端到端直接合成基线，同时将 LLM token 使用量降低高达 35%。

Conclusion: 生成的内核是独立验证子内核的程序化组合，具有可配置性（子内核实现可替换）、可解释性（错误与加速可追溯到特定子内核）以及对相关模型架构的易适应性。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in LLM-based code generation, writing correct and performant kernels for hardware accelerators remains a key bottleneck in scaling modern ML workloads. We present MKEvolve (Modular Kernel Evolve), a framework that iteratively co-evolves a modular decomposition of complex PyTorch modules and the LLM-generated kernel for each submodule, refining the decomposition by splitting and fusing across iterations while independently improving each subkernel via LLM-driven beam search. The resulting kernels are programmatic compositions of independently verified subkernels, making them configurable (subkernel implementations are swappable), interpretable (errors and speedups are traceable to specific subkernels), and readily adaptable to related model architectures. Experiments with Triton on KernelBench L2 and L3, spanning multi-operator sequences and full model architectures, show that MKEvolve improves both correctness and speedup over end-to-end direct synthesis baselines while reducing LLM token usage by up to 35%.

</details>


### [110] [Inducing Comparability of Factorised Probability Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20502)
*Jan Speller,Malte Luttermann,Marcel Gehrke,Tanya Braun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种扩展方案，将两个定义在不同变量集上的概率图模型提升到公共可测空间，利用条件均匀（拉普拉斯）扩展补全不匹配部分，保持概率语义不变，从而允许应用定义良好的分布差异度量，并给出最小结构扩展算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概率图模型比较方法要求模型定义在相同的变量集上，但实际应用中常面临变量集不一致的问题，因此需要一种原则性的方法将模型提升到公共可测空间以便比较。

Method: 设计扩展方案，对任意两个给定模型，使用条件均匀（拉普拉斯）扩展补全未匹配分量，使得联合分布仅与原始分布相差乘法常数且在投影下一致；建立扩展后的联合分布在投影下的不变性；提供将两个因子图扩展到最小公共可测空间及公共图结构的确定性算法。

Result: 证明了扩展后的联合分布在投影下不变，保持了概率语义，使得分布差异度量可被应用；给出了最小结构扩展算法；讨论了结构与测度论性质，识别出比较方法的有希望的标准。

Conclusion: 该工作为异构变量集上的概率图模型比较提供了严格的数学基础和算法框架，有望推动模型比较、集成及迁移学习等领域的发展。

Abstract: To allow for principled comparison between two probabilistic graphical models defined over non-identical variable sets, they have to be lifted to a common measurable space. To this end, we propose an extension scheme for any two given models and establish the formal foundation: Unmatched components are completed using conditionally uniform (Laplace) extensions such that the resulting joint distributions differ from the original ones only by multiplicative constants and coincide under projection. This preserves the probabilistic semantics while enabling the application of well-defined distributional discrepancy measures. We establish the invariance of the induced joint under projection and use the extensions to provide a minimal structural extension of two factor graphs to the smalles common measurable space as well as to a common graphical structure by a deterministic algorithm. In addition, we discuss structural and measure-theoretic properties and identify promising criteria for comparison methodologies.

</details>


### [111] [LeanFlow: A Case Study in Workflow-Driven Lean Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20503)
*Lazar Milikic,Simon Guilloud,Khanh Nguyen,Viktor Kuncak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 LeanFlow，一种将数学论文翻译为可构建 Lean 项目的 LLM 代理系统，并通过在两篇未形式化数学论文（数论与测度论）上的案例研究，对比 Kimi2.6 与 GPT5.5 在不同工作流消融下的完成率、API 调用次数、Token 消耗等指标，验证了完整工作流在预算内完成任务且 Token 成本最低，同时在 RLM25 PFR 切片达 75.7% BEq+ 并解决 ICML 2026 AI for Math TCS 全部 5 个挑战项目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 verifier-in-the-loop 系统虽能生成大型形式化产物，但尚不清楚运行时机制（如队列、工具集、证明工作流）如何影响文档级形式化的完成度、可审计性与效率。

Method: 设计 LeanFlow 代理系统，针对两篇未形式化数学论文进行案例研究；采用模型消融（Kimi2.6、GPT5.5）、证明工作流消融、工具集消融，记录任务结果、API 调用数、输入/输出 Token 数；以 2000 次调用为预算上限。

Result: Kimi2.6 下完整工作流在预算内完成两个文档级项目，无队列变体耗尽预算；GPT5.5 下所有文档级变体均完成，完整工作流在两篇论文上输入 Token 成本最低或并列最低。补充基准：LeanFlow 在 RLM25 PFR 切片达 75.7% BEq+，并在 GPT5.5 运行中解决 ICML 2026 AI for Math TCS 全部 5 个挑战项目。

Conclusion: LeanFlow 证明了结合队列机制的完整工作流能在有限预算内高效完成文档级数学形式化，且在更强模型（GPT5.5）下 Token 成本最优；系统在外部基准上亦表现优异，显示出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: We present and evaluate LeanFlow, an LLM agent system specialized for translating mathematical papers into buildable Lean projects. Recent verifier-in-the-loop systems show that large formal artifacts can be produced, but it remains unclear which runtime mechanisms affect completion, auditability, or efficiency in document-to-project formalization. We study this question through case studies on two previously unformalized mathematical papers in number theory and measure theory, using model, proof-workflow, and toolset ablations with Kimi2.6 and GPT5.5; we report task outcome, API calls, input tokens, and output tokens. With Kimi2.6, the full workflow completes both document-level projects within the 2000-call budget, while no-queue variants reach the budget limit; with GPT5.5, all document-level variants complete, and the full workflow has the lowest or tied-lowest input-token cost on both sources. As complementary calibration, LeanFlow reaches 75.7% BEq+ on the PFR slice of RLM25 and solves all five ICML 2026 AI for Math TCS challenge projects in our GPT5.5 runs.

</details>


### [112] [Optimizing Hypergraph-Based RAG: Toward Better Fact Extraction and Chunk Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20506)
*Houda Khrouf,Pedro Fillastre,Sebastiao Correia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出改进HyperGraphRAG：采用自一致性提示提升LLM抽取准确性，并利用超图上的个性化PageRank算法优化块检索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GraphRAG难以处理n元事实；HyperGraphRAG虽引入超图增强语义，但依赖易出错的LLM抽取和低效的标准块检索。

Method: 1. 使用自一致性提示策略改进LLM抽取过程，降低抽取错误。2. 在超图结构上应用个性化PageRank算法，替代标准块检索，提升检索相关性与效率。

Result: 通过改进抽取和检索两个关键环节，显著提升了HyperGraphRAG的整体准确性和检索性能（具体指标未在摘要中给出）。

Conclusion: 自一致性提示与超图个性化PageRank的结合有效解决了HyperGraphRAG中抽取易错、检索低效的问题，为基于超图的RAG系统提供了更鲁棒的实现路径。

Abstract: GraphRAG enables deeper reasoning by structuring knowledge as graphs but struggles with n-ary facts. HyperGraphRAG uses hypergraphs for richer semantics, improving accuracy, yet relies on error-prone LLM extraction and inefficient standard chunk retrieval. We address this by employing self-consistency prompting to improve the extraction, and Personalized PageRank algorithm over hypergraph to enhance chunk retrieval.

</details>


### [113] [MiniCache: Reusable Program Caching with Small Model Interfaces for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20507)
*Jingquan Chen,Jinghua Piao,Jie Feng,Shaogang Hu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MiniCache是一个可重用程序缓存框架，将Program-of-Thought程序转化为参数化缓存对象，利用小模型进行语义变量提取和推测性起草，降低大模型调用成本，实现高达3.1倍延迟降低和2.8倍吞吐率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在程序辅助推理、代理决策和结构化任务执行中应用广泛，但推理成本高昂，需要一种机制在保持任务质量的同时减少昂贵的大模型调用。

Method: 提出MiniCache框架，将PoT程序转化为参数化缓存对象，跨结构相似请求复用计算；使用同一小模型进行语义变量提取（缓存命中时）和推测性起草（目标大模型生成期间），减少目标大模型调用。

Result: 在购物风格请求数据集、WebShop、Formula和CodeTAT-QA上实验表明，MiniCache改善了推理延迟、缓存复用和准确性的权衡，实现高达3.1倍更低延迟和2.8倍更高吞吐率（并行服务下）。

Conclusion: 小模型不应作为大模型的替代品，而应作为轻量级接口模型，实现可靠高效的可重用程序缓存。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for program-aided reasoning, agentic decision making, and structured task execution, but these applications often incur high inference cost. We present MiniCache, a reusable program caching framework that transforms Program-of-Thought (PoT) programs into parameterized cache objects, enabling reusable computation across structurally similar requests. MiniCache reuses the same small model for semantic variable extraction on cache-hit requests and speculative drafting during target-LLM generation, reducing expensive target-LLM invocations while preserving task quality. Experiments on shopping-style request datasets, WebShop, Formula, and CodeTAT-QA demonstrate that MiniCache improves the trade-off between inference latency, cache reuse, and accuracy, achieving up to 3.1x lower latency and 2.8x higher throughput under parallel serving. These results show that small models are most effective not as replacements for large models, but as lightweight interface models that enable reliable and efficient reusable program caching.

</details>


### [114] [Telco-GAIA: Bilingual Benchmark for Agents in Telecom Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20510)
*Dmitrii Khizbullin,Zaid Alyafeai,Abdelrahman Eldesokey,Nourah AlSultan,Raghad Alshalan,David R. Pugh,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Telco-GAIA 是一个双语多模态基准，用于评估工具使用代理在真实电信运营商数据上的表现，包含 100 个人工验证的问答任务，涉及平均 4.2 跳的多跳推理，跨越静态网站快照、合成 SQL 数据库和外部网络存档三个异构源，涵盖文本、图像和表格模态。基准提供沙箱 Docker 环境，采用归一化精确字符串匹配评分，确保评估客观、确定且可复现。实验显示即使最强模型也仅解决 71% 任务，在成本预算下降至约 40%，视觉接地类别最弱，后端平均得分低于 30%，文档和图像理解仍有巨大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏真实企业级数据、多模态异构源、双语支持以及可复现的确定性评估机制，亟需一个严格、可复现的测试床来评估企业级代理在复杂真实场景下的工具使用与多跳推理能力。

Method: 构建包含静态网站快照（HTML、图像、链接 PDF）、合成关系型 SQL 数据库和外部网络存档三个异构数据源；设计 100 个经人工验证的英语/阿拉伯语双语问答任务，要求平均 4.2 跳的多跳推理；以沙箱 Docker 环境交付基准；采用归一化精确字符串匹配进行评分，消除 LLM-as-a-Judge 的主观性，实现客观、确定、可随时间复现的评估。

Result: 在十二个商业及开源 LLM 上评估专用参考代理：最强模型仅解决 71% 任务；引入中等成本预算后解决率降至约 40%；视觉接地类别表现最弱，后端平均得分低于 30%，表明文档理解和图像理解仍有大量提升空间。

Conclusion: Telco-GAIA 为企业级代理提供了一个严格、可复现的测试床，同时也为构建闭域基准提供了可复用的模板。

Abstract: We introduce Telco-GAIA, a bilingual, multi-modal benchmark for evaluating tool-using agents on the data of a real-world telecommunications operator. Telco-GAIA comprises 100 human-verified question-answering tasks, in English and Arabic, that each demand multi-hop reasoning (4.2 hops on average) over three heterogeneous sources: a static website snapshot (HTML, images, and linked PDFs), a synthetic relational SQL database, and external web archives, spanning text, image, and tabular modalities. The benchmark is delivered as a sandboxed Docker environment and scored by normalized exact string matching, making evaluation objective, deterministic, and reproducible over time without any LLM-as-a-Judge. Evaluating a purpose-built reference agent across twelve commercial and open LLMs, we find Telco-GAIA challenging: even the strongest model solves only 71% of tasks; under a moderate cost budget, this falls to about 40%, and the visually grounded categories remain the weakest, where the average backend scores below 30%, leaving substantial headroom in document and image understanding. Telco-GAIA offers a rigorous, reproducible testbed for enterprise agents and a template for constructing closed-domain benchmarks.

</details>


### [115] [SiGMA: Sign-Guided Merging and Adaptation for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20511)
*Keonhee Park,Gunhee Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SiGMA 框架解决多模态大语言模型在持续指令微调中的负面干扰问题，通过符号引导的自适应训练和推理时合并，显著减轻灾难性遗忘并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态持续指令微调方法主要关注灾难性遗忘，但忽视了推理阶段的负面干扰，即新学习的更新覆盖了有用的先验知识，导致整体性能下降。

Method: 提出 SiGMA 框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 符号引导自适应调优，在训练时减少与历史知识的冲突，以最小漂移学习当前任务；2) 符号引导合并，在推理时选择性缩放显著参数，保留并放大任务特定知识。

Result: 在 UCIT 和 DCL 基准上实验表明，SiGMA 显著降低负面干扰，性能超越现有最先进的多模态持续指令微调方法。

Conclusion: SiGMA 通过符号引导的训练与推理双重机制，有效缓解多模态大模型持续学习中的负面干扰，为后续研究提供了简单高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) is crucial for adapting Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to evolving a sequence of downstream tasks. Prior methods mostly utilize Mixture of Experts or expansion merge approach, primarily focusing on catastrophic forgetting, yet they still suffer from negative interference during inference, where newly learned updates overwrite useful prior knowledge and degrade overall performance. To address this, we propose SiGMA (Sign Guided Merging and Adaptation), a simple yet effective framework that mitigates negative interference with two components: sign guided adaptive tuning during training and sign guided merging at inference. Sign guided adaptive tuning reduces collisions with past knowledge and learns the current task with minimal drift, mitigating severe forgetting. Sign guided merging further improves consolidation by selectively scaling salient parameters to preserve and amplify useful task specific knowledge. Experiments on UCIT and DCL benchmarks show that SiGMA significantly reduces negative interference and outperforms state of the art MCIT methods. Our code is available at SiGMA.

</details>


### [116] [Reliability-Aware LLM Alignment from Inconsistent Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20515)
*Jingyi Huang,Ruohan Zong,Yujun Feng,Liran Ma,Lanyu Shang,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可靠性引导偏好优化（RGPO）框架，通过估计标注者可靠性并推断潜在真实标签，解决RLHF中人类反馈不一致和主观性导致的对齐问题，在LLM对齐基准上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF在LLM对齐中至关重要，但人类标注的固有不一致性和主观性损害其效果。现有方法如DPO将高分歧偏好对与一致共识对同等对待，导致模型过拟合不一致监督信号，产生次优对齐。

Method: 提出RGPO框架：1）估计标注者可靠性并从噪声反馈推断潜在真实标签以识别稳健偏好；2）引入可靠性感知一致性优化，根据标注共识水平动态调节训练目标，使模型优先学习高共识监督信号。

Result: 在LLM对齐基准上的大量实验表明，RGPO有效降低训练数据的不一致性和噪声，相比广泛采用的RLHF基线实现更优性能。代码和配置已开源。

Conclusion: RGPO通过建模标注者可靠性和动态调节优化目标，鲁棒地处理不一致人类反馈，为LLM对齐提供更可靠的偏好优化范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is critical for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with human preferences. However, its efficacy is often compromised by the inherent inconsistency and subjectivity of human annotations. Existing preference optimization frameworks, such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), typically treat ambiguous pairs with high annotator disagreement identically to those with unanimous consensus, forcing models to overfit to inconsistent supervision signals and leading to suboptimal alignment. In this work, we propose Reliability-Guided Preference Optimization (RGPO), a robust framework designed to mitigate the impact of inconsistent human feedback. RGPO estimates annotator reliability and infers latent ground truth labels from noisy human feedback to identify robust preferences. Furthermore, we introduce a reliability-aware consistency optimization that dynamically modulates the training objective based on the consensus level of annotations, ensuring the model prioritizes high-consensus supervision signals. Extensive experiments on LLM alignment benchmarks demonstrate that RGPO effectively reduces inconsistency and noise in training data and achieves superior performance compared to widely adopted RLHF baselines. Our code and configurations are available at https://github.com/GenieHuang/RGPO.

</details>


### [117] [CANN Bench: Benchmarking Agent Generated Kernels against Real NPU and Algorithmic Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20518)
*Xue-Jian Gao,Deng Pan,Yueming Su,Jiasheng Li,Bin Du,Fengming Zhu,Chengdi Ma,Junyi Fan,Qichen Liao,Chengqiu Hu,Xinxian Chen,Lingchao Zheng,Jun Li,Jiwei Yang,Yuwei Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CANN Bench，首个面向华为昇腾 NPU 的 AI 生成算子代码开放基准，包含 53 个算子、1060 个测例、四大难度梯度与多精度格式，采用“编译-正确性-性能”三维加权复合评分，并引入硬件锚定性能上限（HAP）与开箱即用 PyTorch 基线，构建抗奖励作弊、可长期社区共建的量化评测体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算子生成基准几乎只覆盖 CUDA/Triton，缺乏对华为昇腾等非主流硬件编程模型的通用评估基线，导致 AI Agent 在昇腾平台上的内核编写、编译、迭代优化能力难以被量化、复现与持续跟踪。

Method: 1. 构建分层基准集：53 算子、1060 测例，按元素级、MoE 分发、FlashAttention 等四大难度分级，覆盖 FP16/BF16/FP32/INT8。2. 三维加权复合评分：编译通过率、功能正确性、性能得分独立计算再加权。3. 性能分级：以 PyTorch-on-Ascend 开箱即用为基线，以解析得到的硬件锚定性能上限（HAP）为上界，确保反映真实优化空间。4. 评测工具链内置防奖励作弊机制，并纳入官方 CANN 仓库版本管理，支持社区长期共建。

Result: CANN Bench 已开源并集成至官方 CANN 仓库，提供可复现、可持续维护的昇腾生态算子编写能力量化标尺，填补了非 CUDA 硬件平台 AI 生成内核评测的空白。

Conclusion: CANN Bench 通过多维度、硬件感知、抗作弊的评测体系，为昇腾 NPU 上的 AI 算子生成 Agent 提供了首个标准化、长期可演进的基准，推动非主流硬件生态在大模型推理加速领域的工程落地与学术研究。

Abstract: AI agents are now capable of writing, compiling, and iteratively optimizing low-level operator kernels on different hardware platforms. Existing benchmarks, however, focus almost exclusively on CUDA and Triton, leaving hardware ecosystems with less-exposed programming models without a common evaluation baseline.
