Since per-token latency is this cost divided by acceptance length, at matched acceptance end-to-end decode latency improves by the same amount, and more where windowing also lifts acceptance, while preserving the target's verified output distribution. Finally, the unread draft KV -- 7.7-11% of total KV at 1M -- is reclaimed via a compact ring buffer at no acceptance or quality cost.

</details>


### [289] [Semantic-Aware Task Clustering for Constructive and Cooperative Multi-Tasking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21426)
*Ahmad Halimi Razlighi,Maximilian H. V. Tillmann,Edgar Beck,Bho Matthiesen,Armin Dekorsy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出语义感知任务聚类方法用于协作多任务语义通信，通过两阶段优化（层次密度聚类+簇内端到端联合训练）缓解破坏性合作和负迁移，提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作多任务语义通信中，任务间语义关系决定合作是建设性还是破坏性；需确保建设性合作，避免负迁移。

Method: 两阶段顺序优化：1) 短暂初始训练后，利用层次密度空间聚类进行语义任务聚类；2) 在发现的簇内进行端到端联合训练。

Result: 仿真表明该框架有效缓解破坏性合作和负迁移，相比无聚类多任务和单任务训练基线取得准确率提升。

Conclusion: 语义感知任务聚类能确保协作多任务语义通信中的建设性合作，提升任务执行性能。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-task semantic communication (CMT-SemCom) improves task execution performance by leveraging shared representations. However, as we demonstrated in [1], cooperative multi-tasking can be either constructive or destructive, depending on the semantic relationships among tasks. To ensure constructive cooperation, we propose a semantic-aware task clustering method for CMT-SemCom. We have formulated a sequential multi-stage optimization problem in which semantically aligned tasks are clustered once after a short initial training phase, and then end-to-end (E2E) joint training is conducted exclusively within the discovered groups. Specifically, the problem decomposes into two stages: (i) a semantic clustering problem leveraging hierarchical density-based spatial clustering, and (ii) an intra-cluster E2E CMT-SemCom learning problem. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively mitigates destructive cooperation and negative transfer, yielding accuracy gains compared to unclustered multi-tasking and individual training baselines.

</details>


### [290] [Context-weighted Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21427)
*Daniil Cherniavskii,Daniel Severo,Karen Ullrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散流匹配的生成建模改进方法，通过引入局部上下文信息修改连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC），设计了上下文加权采样器和缩放交叉熵损失函数，显著提升了生成质量并降低了困惑度（OpenWebText上最高降低63%），同时保持了任意顺序生成的灵活性，达到了半自回归块扩散基线的同等质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离散流匹配的因子化训练目标会让模型面临难度各异的目标 token，混合了易预测的低熵 token 和高熵的模糊 token。实验发现每个 token 的不确定性与其邻域可用上下文的密度密切相关，因此引入局部上下文信息有望改善训练和采样效率。

Method: 在底层连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）中融入局部上下文信息，提出两项简单修改：1) 上下文加权采样器，利用邻域上下文密度加权采样过程，几乎不增加计算开销；2) 缩放交叉熵损失函数，根据 token 的上下文不确定性重新加权训练信号。

Result: 在 OpenWebText 数据集上，生成困惑度最高降低 63%；生成质量与强半自回归块扩散基线持平，同时保留了任意顺序生成的能力；上下文加权采样器在极小开销下提升采样质量。

Conclusion: 局部上下文是离散生成建模中的关键因素，简单的上下文感知修改即可显著提升采样和训练效率，为离散流匹配提供了实用的改进方向。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching provides a flexible framework for generative modeling on discrete structures. However, the standard factorized training objective exposes the model to targets of varying difficulty, mixing well-conditioned, predictable tokens with ambiguous, high-entropy ones. We empirically demonstrate that the uncertainty over the value of each token is closely related to the density of available context in its neighborhood. Motivated by this observation, we propose a simple modification to the underlying continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) that incorporates local context information. Our context-weighted sampler improves generation quality with negligible computational overhead, while our scaled cross-entropy loss function reweights the training signal from different tokens and reduces generative perplexity by up to 63% on OpenWebText. Moreover, our approach matches a strong semi-autoregressive block diffusion baseline in quality while retaining the ability to perform generation in any order. These results highlight the role of local context as an important factor in discrete generative modeling and show that simple context-aware modifications can significantly improve both sampling and training efficiency.

</details>


### [291] [KroQuant: Kronecker-Structured Block Transforms for Efficient Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21446)
*Yann Bouquet,Alireza Khodamoradi,Kristof Denolf,Mathieu Salzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 KroQuant，一种用于扩散变换器（DiTs）的 W4A4 后训练量化方法，利用学习的 Kronecker 结构可逆变换在 32 元素块上运行，参数量不到逐通道缩放的一半，且通过小张量核 GEMM 实现，在 MI350 上比 SmoothQuant 快 14%；配合离线 LoRaQ 权重校准，在 PixArt-Σ、SANA、FLUX.1-schnell 上达到更接近 FP 参考的输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiTs 在 W4A4 量化时，激活中的离群值导致严重质量下降。标准解法是应用可逆线性变换，但归一化层强制其在每个去噪步在线运行，使推理计算成本成为关键约束。现有方案在量化精度和在线开销间权衡：逐通道缩放（SmoothQuant）计算便宜但损害精度；固定 Hadamard 变换精度较好但需大块尺寸导致高在线成本；学习的全维可逆变换校准最佳但需每层每步稠密 GEMM，开销过大。

Method: KroQuant 对激活的每个 32 元素块应用学习的 Kronecker 结构可逆变换，参数量不足逐通道缩放的一半。块局部结构以小张量核 GEMM 运行，实测在 MI350 GPU 上量化内核比 SmoothQuant 快 14%。随后用离线 LoRaQ 权重校准吸收残余逐权重量化误差。

Result: 在 PixArt-Σ、SANA、FLUX.1-schnell 三个模型上，以 W4A4 (MXFP4e2) 量化，KroQuant 在 MJHQ-30K 和 SDCI 基准上的输出比 SVDQuant 和 LoRaQ 更接近 FP 参考，同时保持或提升图像质量。

Conclusion: KroQuant 通过 Kronecker 结构可逆变换在极低参数开销和高硬件效率下实现了高精度 W4A4 量化，结合离线 LoRaQ 权重校准，在主流 DiT 模型上达到最优量化质量与推理速度的平衡。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) of diffusion transformers (DiTs) to W4A4 severely degrades output quality, because activations entering each linear layer contain outliers that 4-bit formats cannot represent. The standard fix applies an invertible linear transform to the activations and its inverse to the weights before quantizing both. Normalization layers between blocks force this transform to run online at every denoising step, making its inference computation cost the binding design constraint. Existing options trade quantization quality for inference cost: per-channel scaling (SmoothQuant) is computationally cheap but impacts the magnitude of the channels, which can harm quantization accuracy; fixed Hadamard transforms yield better quantization accuracy but require large block sizes that incur a high online cost; learned full-$d$ invertible transforms calibrate best but entail a prohibitive dense $d \times d$ matrix multiplication (GEMM) per layer per step.
  We propose KroQuant, a PTQ method that applies a learned Kronecker-structured invertible transform to each 32-element block of the activation, storing less than half the parameters of per-channel scaling. The block-local structure runs as small tensor-core GEMMs, and on an MI350 GPU the KroQuant quantizer kernel is up to $14\%$ faster than the SmoothQuant kernel. Offline LoRaQ weight calibration then absorbs the residual per-weight quantization error. On PixArt-$Σ$, SANA, and FLUX.1-schnell at W4A4 (MXFP4e2), KroQuant produces outputs closer to the FP reference than SVDQuant and LoRaQ on MJHQ-30K and SDCI, while preserving or improving image quality.

</details>


### [292] [Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21453)
*Rajiv Shailesh Chitale,Rahul Madhavan,Taneesh Gupta,Deepanway Ghosal,Aravindan Raghuveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 TTEL 的测试时扩展算法，通过 token 级错误定位实现高效推理，显著降低生成 token 成本同时保持甚至提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时扩展方法（如独立采样、顺序多轮优化）缺乏 token 级信用分配，导致有效推理前缀被频繁丢弃，计算效率低下。

Method: TTEL 利用固定或环境反馈，对比有反馈条件下的条件概率与无上下文基线，定位错误发生的 token 步骤，随后截断轨迹并分支新生成，最大化复用有效前缀。

Result: 在 LiveCodeBench 上 Qwen3-8B 实现 pass@64 71.0%，生成 token 约为独立采样一半（360.4k vs 735.0k）；在 AIME-2025 和 HMMT-2025 数学基准上，TTEL 在 Qwen3-8B 和 Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 上均稳健超越竞争基线。

Conclusion: TTEL 通过 token 级错误定位与前缀复用，建立了严格占优的帕累托前沿，为大语言模型复杂推理任务的高效测试时扩展提供了新范式。

Abstract: Scaling inference-time computation has emerged as a reliable method to improve the performance of large language models on complex reasoning and programming tasks. However, standard approaches such as independent sampling and sequential multi-turn refinement operate without token-level credit assignment, resulting in computational inefficiency, since valid reasoning prefixes are frequently discarded. In this work, we introduce Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization (TTEL), an inference-time algorithm that utilizes fixed or environment feedback to perform token-level error localization. By comparing conditional probabilities under informed feedback against a null-context baseline, TTEL isolates the step at which an error occurred. The algorithm then truncates the trajectory and branches a new generation, maximally reusing the valid prefix. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TTEL establishes strictly dominating Pareto frontiers across sequential reasoning domains, measured by pass-at-k vs. generated-token cost. With Qwen3-8B on LiveCodeBench, TTEL attains a pass@64 of 71.0% while generating approximately half as many tokens as independent sampling (360.4k vs. 735.0k). Generalizing to math benchmarks AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025, TTEL cleanly outperforms competing test-time baselines across both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507.

</details>


### [293] [Finite-Sample Coverage Audits for High-Recall Candidate Generation: Certification and Learning-Theoretic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21480)
*Martin Anthony,Kaveh Salehzadeh Nobari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究如何在有限样本下认证高召回率流水线初始阶段的漏报相关项质量，证明必须审计被排除池，并给出最优标签复杂度下界，开发了基于二项分布和超几何分布反演的精确有限样本工具箱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实证流水线中，初始高召回阶段决定哪些项目进入后续审查、标注或建模；被遗漏的相关项目将永久丢失。需要量化认证漏报相关项质量所需的审计标签数量，以提供有限样本有效性保证。

Method: 理论分析证明仅使用候选集内标签无法认证非平凡漏报界，必须抽样被排除池；建立有限语料库下的匹配下界，证明任何有效审计需检查约 N0/m 个被排除池标签；开发基于二项分布和超几何分布反演的精确有限样本工具箱，支持漏报质量认证、通过双池设计转换为召回率、同时认证预指定嵌套候选生成器族、生成针对声明扰动机制的压力测试证书。

Result: 证明被排除池审计在零漏报 regime 下是极小最大速率最优的；工具箱提供精确有限样本保证，可结合可观测审查负担选择满足漏报目标且负担最小的预指定候选生成器；所有保证要求候选生成器、预指定族及审计规则在查看认证标签前固定。

Conclusion: 论文从根本上表征了漏报质量认证的标签复杂度，确立了被排除池审计的必要性与最优性，并提供了一套实用的精确有限样本认证框架，可指导实际流水线中候选生成器的选择与审计设计。

Abstract: An initial high-recall stage in an empirical pipeline decides which items pass to later review, labelling, or modelling, and relevant items it misses are lost to every subsequent stage. We study how many audit labels are needed to certify, with finite-sample validity, that this missed relevant mass is small, and our main results characterise the label complexity of this problem. We first show that no procedure using only labels from inside the candidate set can certify any non-trivial bound on the missed mass: the audit must sample the excluded pool, the only region where unrecovered relevant items can lie. We then prove a matching finite-corpus lower bound. Any valid audit that certifies fewer than $m$ missed relevant items with high probability when none are present, even if adaptive and permitted to label the entire included pool, must inspect on the order of $N_0/m$ excluded-pool labels. Excluded-pool auditing is therefore minimax rate-optimal, not merely convenient, for missed-mass certification in the zero-miss regime. Building on this characterisation, we develop an exact finite-sample toolkit, using binomial and hypergeometric inversion rather than asymptotic approximation, that certifies missed mass, converts it to recall through a two-pool design, certifies pre-specified families of nested candidate generators simultaneously, and produces stress-test certificates against declared perturbation mechanisms. These certificates can be paired with observable review burden to select the least burdensome pre-specified candidate generator meeting a missed-mass target. Every guarantee holds under one discipline: the candidate generator, or the pre-specified family from which it is selected, and the audit rule are fixed before the certification labels are examined.

</details>


### [294] [Compact Latent Coordination for Autonomous Vehicles at Unsignalized Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21488)
*Gil Lifshits,Igal Bilik,Gilad Katz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MAPS (Master-Agent Proto-plan System)，一种用于无信号灯路口多自动驾驶车辆协调的层级深度强化学习架构。中心化 Master 生成紧凑连续的 proto-plan 嵌入编码全局协调策略，去中心化 Worker 结合局部观测执行车辆级控制，将战略意图与战术执行解耦。在 HighwayEnv 72 种路口配置上实现零碰撞通行且显著缩短平均通行时间，超越 SOTA 基线；且展现强零样本泛化：3 智能体训练的模型在 5 智能体场景下仍达 94% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MARL 在无信号灯路口协调时面临组合动作空间爆炸、依赖特权信息或智能体设计僵化等难题，难以实现可扩展、鲁棒的多车协同。

Method: 提出层级 DRL 架构 MAPS：中心化 Master 智能体输出紧凑连续的 proto-plan 嵌入表示全局协调意图；去中心化 Worker 智能体融合 proto-plan 与局部观测生成车辆级控制动作。该设计将战略层（协调意图）与战术层（执行控制）解耦，支持各模块独立优化。

Result: 在 HighwayEnv 72 种路口配置实验中，MAPS 实现零碰撞通行，平均通行时间显著降低，性能超越最先进基线。泛化测试显示：仅用 3 智能体训练的模型，零样本部署到 5 智能体场景仍保持 94% 成功率，验证 proto-plan 机制的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于 proto-plan 的层级学习框架为多车辆协调提供了一个可扩展、可泛化的有前景范式，有效解决了组合动作空间、特权信息依赖和刚性设计等核心痛点。

Abstract: Coordinating autonomous vehicles at unsignalized intersections remains a critical challenge for multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems, which typically struggle with combinatorial action spaces, reliance on privileged information, or rigid agent designs. We propose Master-Agent Proto-plan System (MAPS), a hierarchical deep reinforcement learning (DRL) architecture in which a centralized Master agent generates a compact, continuous embedding, denoted as proto-plan, that encodes a global coordination strategy. Decentralized Worker agents integrate this embedding with local observations to execute vehicle-specific control, decoupling strategic intent from tactical execution and enabling independent optimization of each module.
  As a proof-of-concept evaluation of this coordination mechanism, we test MAPS across 72 intersection configurations in HighwayEnv. MAPS achieves collision-free navigation while significantly reducing average travel time, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. The learned proto-plans further exhibit robust generalization: a system trained with three agents achieves a 94% success rate when deployed zero-shot to five-agent scenarios, confirming that proto-plan-based hierarchical learning provides a promising framework for multi-vehicle coordination.

</details>


### [295] [Zero-Flow Two-Sample Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21542)
*Yakun Wang,Leyang Wang,Song Liu,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于零流判据的零流差异(ZFD)及零流双样本检验(ZF2ST)，通过学习两分布样本的局部错位方向模式作为分布差异证据，分离见证函数学习与假设评估，利用神经网络保持统计校准有效性，实验显示在结构性分布变化下检测功效强且I型错误校准良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双样本检验方法在处理结构性分布差异时可能缺乏功效，且难以兼顾灵活建模与统计校准。本文旨在开发一种既能利用神经网络灵活捕捉局部错位模式，又能保持严格统计校准的检验方法。

Method: 构建基于零流判据的统计差异度量ZFD，提出ZF2ST检验流程。核心思想是学习两分布样本间的局部错位方向模式作为见证函数，将见证函数学习与假设评估分离，开发基于回归和功效最大化两种见证学习策略，结合灵活神经网络实现。

Result: 在合成数据和图像数据集上的实验表明，ZF2ST能针对结构性分布变化获得强检测功效，同时保持I型错误率良好校准。

Conclusion: ZF2ST通过零流差异和见证学习分离框架，实现了灵活神经网络建模与有效统计校准的统一，为双样本检验提供了新范式。

Abstract: We propose a new approach to two-sample testing for deciding whether two sets of samples are drawn from the same distribution. The test is built on a statistical discrepancy based on the zero-flow criterion, termed zero-flow discrepancy (ZFD). We prove the validity of ZFD and propose a practical testing procedure, termed the zero-flow two-sample test (ZF2ST). The key idea is to learn how samples from the two distributions are locally misaligned and use the resulting directional pattern as evidence of distributional difference. By separating witness learning from hypothesis evaluation, ZF2ST can use flexible neural networks while maintaining valid statistical calibration. We develop both regression-based and power-maximized approaches for learning the witness. Experiments on synthetic and image datasets demonstrate that ZF2ST can achieve strong testing power for structured distributional changes while maintaining well-calibrated type-I error.

</details>


### [296] [X$^3$-OPD: Distilling Reasoning into Large Audio-Language Models via On-Policy Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21550)
*Dongjie Fu,Di Cao,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Wenxu Jia,Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Yu Zhang,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 X³-OPD，一种跨模态在线策略蒸馏框架，将文本大模型的推理能力迁移到音频-语言模型，通过三层对称语料库构建，显著提升音频接地推理和思维链质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频-语言模型在听觉感知上取得进展，但在深度逻辑推理上落后于文本大模型，主要原因是缺乏高质量音频推理数据。

Method: 提出 X³-OPD 跨模态在线策略蒸馏框架：学生模型基于自身声学感知生成推理轨迹，教师模型使用匹配的文本输入和验证答案提供 token 级指导；构建三层对称语料库，涵盖文本推理转语音、复杂声学场景中的音频事件推理、包含副语言线索的口语对话推理。

Result: 在 MMSU、MMAU、BIG Bench Audio 和 MMAR 基准上实验表明，X³-OPD 显著提升音频接地推理和思维链质量，同时在领域偏移下基本保持模型原有能力。

Conclusion: X³-OPD 有效弥合了音频-语言模型与文本大模型在推理能力上的差距，将跨模态蒸馏扩展到非语言事件、韵律和对话上下文的推理，为音频推理任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: While large audio-language models have achieved remarkable progress in auditory perception, they still lag behind text-based large language models in deep logical reasoning, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality audio reasoning data. To bridge this gap, we propose X$^3$-OPD, a cross-modal on-policy distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from a powerful text teacher to an audio-language student. During training, the student generates reasoning trajectories conditioned on its own acoustic perception, while the teacher provides token-level guidance using matched textual inputs and verified answers. We further construct a three-tier symmetric corpus covering textual reasoning rendered into speech, audio-event reasoning grounded in complex acoustic scenes, and spoken-dialogue reasoning involving paralinguistic cues. This design extends cross-modal distillation beyond textually recoverable content to reasoning grounded in non-linguistic events, prosody, and conversational context. Experiments on MMSU, MMAU, BIG Bench Audio, and MMAR demonstrate that X$^3$-OPD substantially improves audio-grounded reasoning and chain-of-thought quality while largely preserving the model's existing capabilities under domain shift.

</details>


### [297] [Graph Learning on Ensembles of Cyclic Peptides: An Investigation of Molecular Ensemble Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21561)
*Aaron Feller,Kris Deibler,Maxim Secor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了EnsembleEGNN，一种分子集合基础模型，通过共享EGNN层编码每个构象，再用Set Attention Block池化构象表示，利用多任务自监督目标在CREMP数据集上预训练，显著提升了环肽性质预测性能。预训练模型达到R²=0.477，Pearson r=0.699，优于仅序列的BERT基线；与BERT序列编码器联合训练后进一步提升至R²=0.538，Pearson r=0.737，证明了将构象集合编码为热力学信息嵌入的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测通常使用单一代表性构象，但许多分子在溶液中以构象集合形式存在。现有方法忽略了构象集合的热力学信息，导致预测性能受限。需要一种能够编码构象集合并捕捉热力学加权信息的模型来提升分子性质预测精度。

Method: 提出EnsembleEGNN模型：1) 使用共享的等变图神经网络（EGNN）层分别编码每个构象；2) 利用Set Attention Block池化构象表示得到集合嵌入；3) 在CREMP环肽集合数据集上采用多任务自监督预训练，包括掩码token恢复、噪声坐标重构和成对距离重构三个目标；4) 预训练后在下游任务上微调，并探索与序列BERT编码器的端到端联合训练。

Result: 从头训练EnsembleEGNN完全失败（R²=0.005）。预训练模型在CREMP-CycPeptMPDB数据集达到R²=0.477，Pearson r=0.699，优于仅序列BERT基线（R²=0.439，Pearson r=0.667）。与BERT序列编码器端到端联合训练的混合模型进一步提升至R²=0.538，Pearson r=0.737。

Conclusion: 将构象集合编码为单一热力学信息嵌入能显著改善环肽性质预测。预训练对于集合模型学习有效表示至关重要，且结合序列信息的混合模型能进一步提升性能，验证了构象集合编码在分子性质预测中的价值。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction from structure often uses a single representative conformation, even though many molecules exist as conformational ensembles in solution. We introduce EnsembleEGNN, a molecular ensemble foundation model that encodes an ensemble by first encoding each conformer with shared Equivariant Graph Neural Network (EGNN) layers, then pooling the resulting conformer representations with a Set Attention Block. We pretrain the model on CREMP, a cyclic peptide ensemble dataset, using a multi-task self-supervised objective combining masked token recovery, noisy-coordinate reconstruction, and pairwise distance reconstruction. On the CREMP-CycPeptMPDB dataset, training EnsembleEGNN from scratch fails entirely ($R^2=0.005$). However, the pretrained model reaches $R^2=0.477$ and Pearson $r=0.699$, outperforming the sequence-only BERT baseline ($R^2=0.439$, Pearson $r=0.667$). When EnsembleEGNN is co-trained end-to-end with the BERT sequence encoder, the hybrid model improves further to $R^2=0.538$ and Pearson $r=0.737$. These results demonstrate that encoding conformational ensembles into a single thermodynamically informed embedding improves cyclic-peptide property prediction.

</details>


### [298] [Beyond Sufficiency: Time Series Explanation with Counterfactual Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21573)
*Hongnan Ma,Yiwei Shi,Mengyue Yang,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimePNS是一个必要性感知的时间序列解释框架，通过反事实干预识别决策关键子序列，改进了充分性-必要性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的充分性导向解释方法可能将高重要性分配给支持预测但非模型决策所必需的虚假子序列，缺乏对必要性的考量。

Method: 采用两阶段设计：阶段I学习可识别的因果生成过程及充分性导向解释掩码；阶段II对时间因素执行反事实干预以获得必要性信号，监督时间门控细化初始解释，抑制非必要成分并强调反事实必要成分。

Result: 在合成和真实世界时间序列基准上的实验表明，TimePNS能更准确地识别决策关键子序列，并在强基线上持续改进充分性-必要性权衡。

Conclusion: TimePNS通过引入反事实干预量化必要性，提供了更忠实的时间序列分类器解释，推动了时间序列可解释性研究。

Abstract: Faithful explanations of time-series classifiers should identify subsequences that are not only sufficient to preserve a black-box model's prediction, but also necessary for maintaining it. However, existing sufficiency-oriented methods can assign high importance to spurious subsequences that support the prediction without being essential to the model's decision. We introduce \textbf{TimePNS}, a necessity-aware framework for time-series explanation. Inspired by Pearl's counterfactual notion of necessity, TimePNS assesses whether a temporal factor is necessary by intervening on it and measuring whether the original prediction is disrupted. The framework adopts a two-stage design. Stage I learns an identifiable causal generative process together with a sufficiency-oriented explanation mask. Stage II performs counterfactual interventions on temporal factors to derive necessity signals, which supervise a temporal gate that refines the initial explanation by suppressing non-essential components and emphasizing counterfactually necessary ones. Experiments on synthetic and real-world time-series benchmarks show that TimePNS more accurately identifies decision-critical subsequences and consistently improves sufficiency-necessity trade-offs over strong baselines.

</details>


### [299] [Expanding Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21585)
*Sophia Tang,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows) 和 Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs)，首次实现了生成模型中输出维度/序列长度的可学习、可控变化，突破了固定维度的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的生成模型仅支持固定维度或固定序列长度，无法直接处理变长输出（如变长序列、变大小图）的生成任务，缺乏将输出尺寸作为可学习自由度的 principled 框架。

Method: 构建 Expanding Interpolant：在状态空间中逐步增广条件噪声，定义不断升维的分布流。设计 Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs)：将任意两个时间步的映射分解为可学习的 Expand Operator（条件增广新坐标/Token）和 Transport Map（沿插值推进扩展后的状态），两者复合得到联合扩展与去噪的单步映射。并将框架推广至离散单纯形，支持变长序列与变大小图生成。

Result: 在连续与离散模态上均验证了 EFlows/EFMs 的有效性，实现了输出尺寸作为可控、可学习自由度的生成，且保持了流模型的快速采样与可控性优势。

Conclusion: EFlows 与 EFMs 为变维度、变长度生成提供了统一、 principled 的理论框架，将现有固定画布流模型作为特例（Expand Operator 为恒等映射）纳入，显著拓展了流模型的适用范围。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models have enabled remarkable progress in fast and controllable generation across continuous and discrete state spaces, yet existing parameterizations are constrained to fixed dimensions or fixed sequence lengths. Here, we introduce Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows), which define flows between distributions of increasing dimensionality along an expanding interpolant that grows the state by augmenting it with conditional noise. Building on this construction, we propose Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs), a new class of flow maps that distill the expanding interpolant into efficient few-step generative models. Each EFM factors the map between any two timesteps into two learnable operations: an expand operator, which augments the state space with new coordinates or tokens conditioned on the current state, and a transport map, which pushes the expanded state forward along the interpolant. Composing these operators yields a single map that jointly expands and denoises the state, recovering existing fixed-canvas flows and flow maps as the special case in which the expand operator is the identity. We further extend the framework to the discrete simplex, enabling variable-size graph generation and variable-length sequence generation. Across both continuous and discrete modalities, we establish EFlows and EFMs as a principled framework for settings in which output size is itself a learned, controllable degree of freedom.

</details>


### [300] [Uncertainty-Aware Trust Estimation for Multi-LLM Systems via Structured Expert Judgement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20529)
*Jiawei Zheng,Jiazhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于不确定性感知的多LLM集成方法，采用Cooke式对数加权机制，通过校准问题评估专家可靠性，在异质和污染专家面板中显著优于简单集成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM集成方法假设所有模型同等可信，忽略了不确定性质量差异，这在异质LLM（可靠性和能力差异大）场景下表现脆弱，易受不可靠或对抗性专家影响。

Method: 将多LLM聚合建模为不确定性感知的信任估计问题，引入决策理论中的结构化专家判断方法，利用上下文感知校准问题基于概率预测质量评估专家可靠性，采用Cooke式对数加权惩罚过度自信的错误预测并奖励校准良好的专家。

Result: 在MMLU和MMLU-Pro上实验表明：同质设置下各聚合方法表现相近；在异质和污染专家面板中，Cooke加权成为关键，实现了更优的准确率-可靠性平衡，并在引入不可靠专家时保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多LLM聚合不仅需要组合预测，更需要在不确定性下校准信任，即引入不确定性感知的信任估计机制。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) ensembles are increasingly used to improve reliability by combining predictions from multiple LLMs. However, existing aggregation methods typically assume that all models are equally trustworthy, overlooking differences in uncertainty quality. This assumption is poorly suited to heterogeneous LLMs, whose reliability and capability vary significantly, making naive aggregation vulnerable to unreliable or adversarial experts. In this work, we formulate multi-LLM aggregation as a problem of uncertainty-aware trust estimation. We adapt structured expert judgment from decision theory, using context-aware calibration questions to estimate expert reliability based on the quality of its probabilistic predictions. Specifically, we employ Cooke-style log weighting, which penalises overconfident incorrect predictions and favours well-calibrated experts. We evaluate our approach on MMLU and MMLU-Pro across homogeneous, heterogeneous, and contaminated expert panels. Results show that while aggregation methods perform similarly in homogeneous settings, Cooke weighting becomes critical under heterogeneity and contamination. It achieves a superior accuracy-reliability balance and remains robust when unreliable experts are introduced. These findings suggest that Multi-LLM aggregation requires not just combining predictions, but calibrating trust under uncertainty.

</details>


### [301] [CLOE: Christoffel Loss Autoencoder for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20530)
*Léa Billet,Louise Travé-Massuyès,Elodie Chanthery,Alexandre Gaffet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLOE 结合自编码器降维与 Christoffel 函数异常检测，设计新损失函数对齐表示学习，实现高维表格数据上优异的轻量级半监督异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Christoffel 函数的方法虽简单且理论基础好，但在高维数据上扩展性差；轻量级方法常难以处理高维数据且需调参多。

Method: CLOE 使用自编码器将高维数据映射到潜在空间，在潜在空间应用 Christoffel 函数检测器；设计基于 Christoffel 函数的新损失函数指导自编码器学习正常数据分布的支撑集；提出原则性阈值设定和高效单超参数调优策略。

Result: 在多个高维表格异常检测基准上，CLOE 优于现有方法，同时保持轻量级和低调参优势。

Conclusion: CLOE 成功解决了 Christoffel 函数方法在高维上的扩展性问题，提供了一种高效、易用的半监督异常检测方案。

Abstract: Semi-supervised anomaly detection plays a key role in diverse fields such as process monitoring, healthcare, and finance. However, lightweight methods often struggle with high-dimensional data and typically require careful tuning of multiple hyperparameters. Among existing approaches, Christoffel Function--based methods are attractive due to their simplicity, requiring at most a single hyperparameter. They also benefit from a well-established theoretical foundation that yields several interesting results for data science. However, their main limitation is poor scalability to high-dimensional settings. In this paper, we introduce CLOE, a new method that combines an autoencoder for dimensionality reduction with a Christoffel Function--based detector applied in the latent space. To better align representation learning with anomaly detection, we design a novel loss function that leverages the Christoffel Function to guide the autoencoder toward representations that better capture the support of the normal data distribution. We further propose a principled procedure to set the detection threshold and an efficient strategy to tune the single remaining hyperparameter. Experiments on multiple high-dimensional tabular anomaly detection benchmarks demonstrate that CLOE achieves superior performance compared to existing methods, while preserving the lightweight and low-tuning advantages of Christoffel Function--based approaches.

</details>


### [302] [Position: Stop Reactively Patching Your Model Every Time and Start Proactive Test-Driven AI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20532)
*Nadine Chang,Maying Shen,Jialiang Wang,Rafid Mahmood,Jose M. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出从被动反应式AI飞轮转向主动测试驱动飞轮，通过建立'测试空间'将反馈数据映射到任务目标，数学证明主动飞轮在长期扩展性上优于被动飞轮，且所需迭代次数更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统需在多样化、开放式用例下运行，当前部署维护管道主要依赖被动反应式飞轮（观察用户行为错误并修补模型），但存在关键局限：忽略错误在系统目标中的广泛上下文，无法预防未来边缘情况，导致不必要的迭代；且因开放世界用例的长尾特性，收集剩余错误在统计上日益困难。

Method: 倡导创建'测试空间'将反馈数据技术性映射到任务目标，推动飞轮从被动转向主动测试驱动模式；并通过数学证明主动飞轮优于被动飞轮。

Result: 数学证明主动测试驱动飞轮在长期扩展性上优于被动反应式飞轮，且达到同等泛化性能所需迭代次数更少。

Conclusion: 为解决被动飞轮局限并逼近可泛化系统，必须采用主动测试驱动飞轮机制。

Abstract: Many modern AI systems are designed to operate under diverse, open-ended, use-cases. To help generalize deployed systems, many deployed-system maintenance pipelines use a reactive AI flywheel that observes emerging feedback from user behavior (errors) and patches the model accordingly. However, when used as the primary maintenance mechanism, these flywheels often ignore the broader context of these errors within the system's objectives, failing to preempt potential future edge cases, which leads to more unnecessary flywheel iterations. Also, it is statistically increasingly difficult to collect remaining errors due to the long-tail nature of open-world use-cases. This position paper argues that a proactive test-driven flywheel is required to address reactive flywheel's limitations and to approach a generalizable system. We advocate for creating a "test space" to technically map feedback data to task objectives, evolving the flywheel from reactive to proactive. We augment our position by mathematically proving a proactive one achieves better long-term scaling with fewer iterations than the reactive flywheel.

</details>


### [303] [Grounding Investor Views: Neural Predicates in the Black-Litterman Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20533)
*Marcos Florencio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于神经谓词的结构化概率方法，用于Black-Litterman模型中的视图生成，将结构化金融分析数据通过神经谓词的组合层级处理，输出概率分布映射到选股矩阵P、视图收益向量q和视图不确定性矩阵Ω，实现了可解释、可微分的端到端学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Black-Litterman模型要求投资者指定资产收益视图及其不确定性估计，但这一过程主要依赖主观判断且难以规模化，缺乏系统化、数据驱动的视图生成机制。

Method: 构建神经谓词的组合层级结构，处理结构化金融分析数据，输出市场立场的概率分布，将其映射为Black-Litterman模型所需的选股矩阵P、视图收益向量q和视图不确定性矩阵Ω；视图置信度由谓词输出分布推导得出，实现端到端可微分学习。

Result: 该方法提供了数据驱动的视图生成替代方案，具有可解释性（投资组合权重可追溯至底层数据的逻辑链）和完全可微分性，支持端到端学习。

Conclusion: 神经谓词框架为Black-Litterman模型的视图生成提供了一个结构化、概率化、可解释且可训练的解决方案，有效解决了传统方法主观性强、难以规模化的问题。

Abstract: Portfolio construction under the Black-Litterman model requires investors to specify views on asset returns alongside explicit uncertainty estimates -- a process that remains largely subjective and difficult to scale. We propose a formal approach in which neural predicates serve as a structured, probabilistic mechanism for view generation. In our formulation, structured financial analysis data is processed through a compositional hierarchy of neural predicates whose outputs -- probability distributions over market stances -- are mapped to the pick matrix $\mathbf{P}$, the view return vector $\mathbf{q}$, and the view uncertainty matrix $\boldsymbolΩ$ of the Black-Litterman model. View confidence is derived from predicate output distributions, providing a data-driven alternative to subjective uncertainty elicitation. The resulting approach is interpretable, in the sense that any portfolio weight can be traced back through the predicate's logical chain to the underlying data, and fully differentiable, enabling end-to-end learning.
